### [1] [Semantic Edge-Cloud Communication for Real-Time Urban Traffic Digital Twins](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.14187)
*Murat Arda Onsu,Arman Dogru,Poonam Lohan,Burak Kantarci,Aisha Syed*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于语义通信的边缘-云协同框架，用于实时城市交通数字孪生，通过传输任务相关语义信息替代原始视频流，显著降低通信开销，同时保持重建精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 城市交通系统日益复杂，需要实时感知、主动碰撞预测和智能管理。现有AI交通管理依赖高质量多场景数据，而数字孪生可提供同步虚拟副本。然而，持续同步高保真数字孪生需要大量边缘-云通信，直接传输原始视频流会带来严重带宽与延迟约束。

Method: 提出面向实时城市交通数字孪生应用的语义通信边缘-云框架，利用边缘智能仅提取并传输任务相关的语义交通信息，而非完整视频流，在保留数字孪生精确重建所需关键动态信息的同时降低通信开销。

Result: 实验结果显示传输节省超过99%，平均轨迹重建误差仅为0.12米。

Conclusion: 该框架被证明是面向AI赋能城市交通数字孪生应用的有效、可扩展且低延迟的解决方案。

Abstract: Urban traffic systems are becoming increasingly complex, demanding real-time traffic perception, proactive collision prediction, and intelligent management to improve safety and reduce congestion. Although AI-driven traffic management has shown strong promise, its success relies on diverse, high-quality data spanning critical scenarios such as collisions, near-collisions, and traffic rule violations. Digital twins offer a powerful solution by enabling synchronized virtual replicas of physical traffic environments for real-time monitoring, collision prediction, traffic-flow optimization, and diverse realistic scenario generation. However, continuously synchronizing a high-fidelity urban traffic digital twin through edge surveillance cameras requires substantial edge-cloud communication, and transmitting raw video streams leads to severe bandwidth and latency constraints. To overcome this challenge, this paper proposes a semantic communication-based edge-cloud framework for real-time urban traffic digital twin applications. By leveraging edge intelligence to extract and transmit only task-relevant semantic traffic information instead of full video feeds, the proposed framework significantly reduces communication overhead while preserving the essential dynamics needed for accurate real-time digital twin reconstruction. Experimental results demonstrate more than 99% transmission savings and an average trajectory reconstruction error of only 0.12 m, confirming the proposed framework as an effective, scalable, and low-latency solution for AI-enabled urban traffic digital twin applications.

</details>


### [2] [An Availability Management Framework for Microservice-Based Safety-Critical CIoT Systems: Architecture and End-to-End Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.14230)
*Hassaan Siddiqui,Ferhat Khendek*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 本文提出可用性管理框架（AMF），通过“监测-预测-恢复”闭环，将机器学习异常检测与“先建后断”备用副本切换相结合，弥补现有编排平台的纯被动恢复缺陷，并在三层远程驾驶测试台上验证其可显著降低端到端服务中断时间。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 关键任务型蜂窝物联网系统日益依赖跨异构边缘与云平台的微服务架构。然而现有编排平台的容错机制主要仍是被动式的，即故障发生后才重启或重调度组件，这会造成数秒级的端到端服务中断时间（E2E SOT），在容器重启退避机制下甚至进一步恶化。

Method: 提出可用性管理框架（AMF），一个通用的“监测-预测-恢复”闭环，在被动恢复之外引入基于机器学习的异常检测与向备用副本的“先建后断”（make-before-break）切换。候选ML模型在离线阶段通过三种协议（含运行工况变化）进行评估，以挑选用于在线部署的模型。AMF在三层远程驾驶（ToD）测试台上以分阶段资源耗尽场景进行实例化与评测。

Result: 相比仅依赖Kubernetes的恢复方式，AMF能够在异常窗口内完成切换，降低E2E SOT，并避免因反复原地重启与退避导致的数十秒级中断。在该非实时测试台上，残余E2E SOT落在健康路径抖动范围内，即受路径时序限制而非恢复决策的滞后性限制。

Conclusion: 将经验证的基于机器学习的异常检测器与主动切换相耦合，可以从关键路径中消除由重启和退避主导的服务中断，从而提升安全关键CIoT系统的可用性。

Abstract: Safety-critical Cellular Internet of Things (CIoT) systems increasingly rely on microservice architectures orchestrated across heterogeneous edge and cloud platforms. Although this paradigm improves modularity and scalability, the fault-tolerance mechanisms of contemporary orchestration platforms remain predominantly reactive, i.e., failed components are restarted or rescheduled only after an outage has already occurred. Such recovery can produce multi-second End to End Service Outage Time (E2E SOT), which can grow further under container restart backoff. This paper proposes an Availability Management Framework (AMF), a generic monitor-predict-recover loop that complements reactive recovery with machine learning-based anomaly detection and make-before-break switchover to a standby replica. Candidate ML models are evaluated offline under three protocols, including a change of operating regime, in order to select a model for live deployment. AMF is instantiated and evaluated on a three-tier tele-operated driving (ToD) testbed under a staged resource-exhaustion scenario. Relative to Kubernetes-only recovery, AMF completes switchover during the anomaly window, reduces E2E SOT, and avoids the tens-of-seconds outages associated with repeated in-place restarts under backoff. On this non-real-time testbed, residual E2E SOT lies inside healthy-path jitter and is therefore limited by path timing rather than by lateness of the recovery decision. The paper whows that coupling a validated ML based anomaly detector with proactive switchover can remove restart- and backoff-dominated outage from a critical path.

</details>


### [3] [Harvesting-Aware Routing for Low-Latency Reliable Transmission in Interference-Powered Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.14250)
*Azadeh Sheikholeslami,Allan Nguyen*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 该论文研究以环境射频干扰为唯一能量来源的多跳无线网络中，在瑞利衰落与端到端中断概率约束下，联合路由与功率分配以最小化时延，提出HOPE与THOPE两种多项式时间最优算法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 能量采集节点仅依赖环境射频干扰供能，干扰具有双重角色：既是可采集的能量来源，又会损害链路可靠性。因此需要在满足目标端到端中断概率的前提下，权衡能量积累与传输可靠性，实现最小化时延的路由与功率分配。

Method: 首先将乘性形式的端到端中断概率约束转化为可加形式。基于此提出HOPE算法，以最小化瓶颈采集时延（路径上最长的能量积累时间）为目标，将问题归约为带干扰感知链路权重的图上最小代价路径问题；THOPE进一步将目标扩展为总端到端时延，加入累积传输时延，同样具有闭式最小代价路径解。两算法均用Dijkstra在多项式时间内求解，并给出最优发射功率的显式表达式。

Result: 仿真表明，与最短路径和最小能量基准相比，HOPE和THOPE可将平均总时延降低至多一个数量级，且时延分布高度集中，对干扰源密度基本不敏感。

Conclusion: 在干扰兼具供能与干扰双重作用的多跳能量采集网络中，通过将中断约束可加化并构造干扰感知链路权重，可在多项式时间内获得时延最优的路由与功率分配方案。

Abstract: We consider a multi-hop wireless network where energy-harvesting nodes rely solely on ambient radio-frequency interference as their power source. The goal is to deliver a packet from source to destination with minimum latency while guaranteeing a target end-to-end outage probability under Rayleigh fading. This gives rise to a joint routing and power allocation problem in which interference plays a dual role: it provides harvestable energy, but also impairs link reliability. We first transform the intricate, multiplicative end-to-end outage constraint into an additive form. Then, we propose two optimal algorithms. The first, HOPE, minimizes the bottleneck harvesting latency, i.e., the longest energy-accumulation time along the path, by reducing the problem to a least-cost path computation on a graph with interference-aware link weights. The second, THOPE, extends the objective to total end-to-end latency, adding the cumulative transmission delay, and likewise admits a closed-form least-cost path solution. Both algorithms are solved in polynomial time via Dijkstra's algorithm and yield explicit expressions for the optimal transmit powers. Extensive simulations reveal that HOPE and THOPE reduce the average total latency by up to an order of magnitude compared with shortest-path and minimum-energy benchmarks, while exhibiting sharply concentrated delay distributions that are largely insensitive to interferer density.

</details>


### [4] [RADIUS-Assisted Companion PSK Discovery: Enabling WPA3-SAE in Externally Authenticated Multi-PSK (ePSK / MPSK / DPSK / iPSK / PPSK) Deployments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.14410)
*Azif Abdul Salam Kolisam Beevi,Anil Kumar Reddy,Niraj Lochan Mahapatra*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: RA-CAPD 将多 PSK 网络中客户端预共享密钥的识别由 AP 本地试算改为通过 RADIUS VSA 转发至外部服务器，实现 WPA3-SAE 与 WPA2-PSK 的平滑接入。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型 MDU、学生宿舍与酒店网络通常不将住户 PSK 存放于 AP，而是存于外部 RADIUS 服务器或与物业管理系统同步的云数据库。此前工作 CAPD 依赖 AP 能自行试算所有 PSK，该假设在此类场景下不成立，因此需要一种由外部服务器完成密钥匹配的机制。

Method: AP 通过 RADIUS 厂商特定属性（VSA）将 ANonce、原始 EAPOL-Key message 2（含 SNonce、MIC、RSNE）、SSID、客户端 MAC、BSSID 与 AP MAC 转发给外部服务器；服务器对所有已配置密钥验证 MIC，识别匹配的 PSK，并以 RADIUS 共享密钥（云数据库场景下为按 AP 密钥）加密返回该 PSK，同时附带单元/房间/用户身份与 VLAN。AP 据此绑定客户端与密钥、身份，并在域内所有 AP 间同步该绑定，然后将客户端迁移至目标配置文件：支持 802.11v 的客户端通过 BSS Transition Management 请求引导，其余客户端通过断连重连迁移。目标配置文件上，SAE 客户端完成 WPA3-SAE，仅支持 WPA2 的客户端完成 WPA2-PSK，无需再次搜索，伴生 BSSID 仅用于入网。

Result: 论文给出了设计、属性字典、算法、复杂度与安全性质，并在商用接入点上完成了实验室验证。

Conclusion: RA-CAPD 去除了 CAPD 对 AP 本地试算 PSK 的依赖，将密钥发现委托给外部服务器，从而在不存储住户 PSK 的 AP 上支持 WPA3-SAE 与多 PSK 接入。

Abstract: Large multi-dwelling unit (MDU), student-housing and hospitality networks rarely store resident pre-shared keys (PSKs) on the access point (AP). Instead, the per-resident keys of ePSK, MPSK, DPSK, iPSK or PPSK services are held in an external RADIUS server or cloud database synchronised with the property-management system. Our earlier work, Companion-BSSID-Assisted PSK Discovery (CAPD), brought WPA3-SAE to multi-PSK networks by learning the client's PSK on a WPA2-only companion BSSID and steering the client with IEEE 802.11v to a hidden WPA2/WPA3 primary BSSID of the same SSID. CAPD assumes that the AP can test every PSK itself. This paper removes that assumption. We propose RADIUS-Assisted Companion PSK Discovery (RA-CAPD). The AP forwards the ANonce, the raw EAPOL-Key message 2 (SNonce, MIC, RSNE), the SSID, the client MAC, the BSSID and the AP MAC to the external server in RADIUS vendor-specific attributes (VSAs). The server validates the MIC against all provisioned keys, identifies the matching PSK, and returns it encrypted under the RADIUS shared secret (or a per-AP key for cloud databases), together with the unit, room or user identity and the VLAN. The AP binds the client to this PSK and identity, synchronises the binding across all APs in the domain, and moves the client to the configured profile: 802.11v-capable clients via a BSS Transition Management request, and other clients by disconnection and reconnection. On that profile, SAE-capable clients complete WPA3-SAE and WPA2-only clients complete WPA2-PSK, both with the learned key and no further search, so the companion BSSID serves only for onboarding. We present the design, attribute dictionary, algorithms, complexity and security properties, together with laboratory validation on commercial access points.

</details>


### [5] [Energy-Aware Agentic Orchestration for Multi-Site Service Deployment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.14793)
*Rodrigo Moreira,Alisson Chaves,Larissa F. Rodrigues Moreira,Tereza Carvalho,Tiago Barros,Daniel Corujo,Rui Aguiar,David Gomez-Barquero,Flávio de Oliveira Silva*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 本文提出能源感知的智能体编排框架，将LLM推理能耗纳入控制回路测量，在多站点Kubernetes测试床对比单次工具调用、ReAct与计划-执行三种策略，发现推理策略与意图复杂度显著影响编排决策的GPU能耗，前缀缓存使ReAct平均GPU能耗降低29.4%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着数据中心承载的AI负载不断增长，跨地理分布式基础设施的能源感知编排日益重要。然而现有方法在系统级可持续性评估中往往排除了自动决策本身的能源成本，忽略了LLM推理带来的开销。

Method: 构建能源感知的智能体编排框架，将LLM推理视为控制回路中可测量的组成部分。在横跨两个欧洲数据中心的多站点Kubernetes测试床上，从配电单元到单Pod能耗归因进行分层仪表化测量。使用十个决策复杂度不同的自然语言编排意图，对比单次工具调用、ReAct和计划-执行三种策略，并评估前缀缓存的影响。

Result: 推理策略与意图复杂度强烈影响编排决策期间的GPU能耗。ReAct和计划-执行在所有评估意图上都能生成可执行的工具调用，但需要多次LLM推理；单次配置成本更低却无法生成可执行动作。前缀缓存使ReAct的平均GPU能耗降低29.4%。在所评估的轻量级工作负载中，1秒测量窗口内观测到的基础设施能耗变化不足以抵消测得的GPU决策能耗。

Conclusion: 研究结果表明，在评估AI原生编排的可持续性时必须计入推理开销，并推动采用能源预算约束下的智能体策略选择。

Abstract: Energy-aware orchestration across geographically distributed infrastructures is increasingly important as data centers absorb growing Artificial Intelligence (AI) workloads. However, existing approaches often exclude the energy cost of automated decision-making from system-level sustainability assessments. This paper presents an energy-aware agentic orchestration framework that treats Large Language Model (LLM) reasoning as a measurable component of the control loop. We compare single-shot tool calling, ReAct, and plan-and-execute strategies on a multi-site Kubernetes testbed spanning two European data centers and instrumented at layers ranging from power distribution units to per-pod energy attribution. Using ten natural-language orchestration intents with varying decision complexity, we show that reasoning strategy and intent complexity strongly influence graphics processing unit (GPU) energy consumption during orchestration decisions. ReAct and plan-and-execute generated executable tool calls across all evaluated intents but required multiple LLM inferences, whereas the single-shot configuration was less costly but non-actionable. Prefix caching reduced mean ReAct GPU energy consumption by 29.4%. For the evaluated lightweight workload, the infrastructure-energy changes observed within the one-second measurement window were insufficient to offset the measured GPU decision energy. These findings demonstrate the importance of accounting for reasoning overhead when assessing the sustainability of AI-native orchestration and motivate energy-budgeted selection of agent strategies.

</details>


### [6] [Stackelberg Game with Multi-Agent Transformer for ISRS-Aware Resource Re-optimization in Multi-Band Optical Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.14830)
*Jiaxin Liu,Rentao Gu,Miao Zhu,Zeguang Zou,Hui Li,Yuefeng Ji*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 提出Stackelberg博弈与多智能体Transformer结合的SG-MAT方法，用于多波段光网络中ISRS感知的资源再优化，在中等负载下实现最高利用率提升并将阻塞率降低约30%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生成式AI推动计算基础设施向互连AI数据中心发展，多波段光网络成为其连接骨干。为应对日益动态的流量需求，网络需持续进行资源再优化以提升资源利用率并降低业务阻塞。然而多波段传输会放大信道间受激拉曼散射（ISRS），在业务间引入非线性耦合：局部调整会扰动全局功率分布，导致业务间优化决策相互依赖；同时非线性作用随所选再优化业务子集而变化，不恰当的子集选择会削弱再优化效果或降低可获得的收益。

Method: 提出Stackelberg博弈与多智能体Transformer（SG-MAT）方法，用于ISRS感知的资源再优化。SG-MAT采用领导者-跟随者结构：基于MAT的跟随者建模非线性耦合并生成协调的信道重分配，将利用率提升反馈给领导者；领导者利用该反馈识别关键业务子集，逼近Stackelberg均衡以指导全局决策，从而实现一致且高效的再优化。

Result: 仿真结果表明，SG-MAT实现了最高的利用率提升，在中等流量负载下将阻塞率降低约30%，且计算复杂度与基准方法相当。

Conclusion: SG-MAT通过领导者-跟随者结构与多智能体Transformer建模ISRS非线性耦合，并识别关键业务子集逼近Stackelberg均衡，能够在可接受的计算复杂度下有效提升多波段光网络的资源利用率并显著降低阻塞率。

Abstract: The rapid expansion of generative AI is driving computing infrastructures toward interconnected AI data centers. Multi-band optical networks have become the backbone of such connectivity. To accommodate increasingly dynamic traffic demands, these networks require continual resource re-optimization to improve resource utilization and reduce service blocking. However, multi-band transmission amplifies inter-channel stimulated Raman scattering(ISRS), inducing nonlinear coupling among services. This coupling complicates re-optimization, as local adjustments perturb the global power profile, creating interdependent optimization decisions across services. Moreover, the nonlinear interactions vary with the subset of services selected for re-optimization, and inappropriate selection may reduce re-optimization effectiveness or diminish achievable benefits. To address these challenges, we propose a Stackelberg Game with Multi-Agent Transformer (SG-MAT) approach for ISRS-aware resource re-optimization. SG-MAT employs a leader-follower structure, in which the MAT-based follower models nonlinear coupling and generates coordinated channel reallocations, feeding the utilization improvements to the leader. Using this feedback, the leader identifies critical service subsets, approximating a Stackelberg equilibrium to guide global decisions and enabling consistent and efficient re-optimization. Simulation results show that SG-MAT achieves the highest utilization improvement and reduces the blocking ratio by approximately 30% under moderate traffic loads, with computational complexity comparable to benchmarks.

</details>


### [7] [Conflict-Aware Autonomous Resource Orchestration for 5G Edge Slicing Using Multi-Agent Systems and LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.14903)
*Nathan R. Prediger,Rodrigo Moreira,Juliano Araujo Wickboldt,Tereza C. Carvalho,Pedro Oliveira,Paulo Novais,Flávio de Oliveira Silva*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 该论文提出一种双时间尺度架构，由LLM策略层将自然语言网络意图转换为经济策略参数，底层由SPADE切片智能体执行Vickrey式第二价格拍卖，并在真实Kubernetes/Open5GS边缘集群上端到端验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 5G边缘网络切片需同时满足两个耦合需求：理解运营商高层意图，并在动态竞争下重新分配稀缺的计算与带宽资源。集中式优化在快速变化的边缘竞争中存在计算与响应性挑战；基于多智能体系统（MAS）的拍卖虽可支持资源仲裁，但仍依赖运营商定义的经济参数与优先级；而将LLM直接嵌入循环控制回路会引入不可忽略的推理延迟与非确定性行为。

Method: 提出双时间尺度架构：宏观尺度上，基于LLM的Strategist将自然语言网络意图转换为经济策略参数（预算、收入、投标激进度）；运行尺度上，由基于SPADE的切片智能体运行确定性的、受Vickrey启发的第二价格机制来仲裁反复出现的资源竞争；最终分配通过直接在User Plane Function pod上修补Kubernetes CPU限制与Calico CNI带宽注解来强制执行。

Result: 在真实Kubernetes/Open5GS边缘集群上，针对两类互补场景各重复30次进行端到端评估。智慧城市场景中，自然语言的公共安全意图在数轮拍卖内即传播为切片投标与带宽分配的可测量变化；分层切片场景中，该机制提供了依赖优先级的资源与QoE差异化，可适应需求的突变，并在保持近乎完整的共享带宽池分配的同时，维持已配置的最小保障。

Conclusion: 双时间尺度架构有效弥合了高层意图理解与动态资源仲裁之间的鸿沟，在真实边缘集群上实现了意图驱动、确定性执行且兼顾优先级与最小保障的5G边缘网络切片资源管理。

Abstract: 5G edge network slicing must reconcile two coupled requirements: understanding high-level operator intent and reallocating scarce computing and bandwidth resources under dynamic contention. Centralized optimization can face computational and responsiveness challenges under rapidly changing edge contention, and Multi-Agent System (MAS)-based auctions can support resource arbitration but still depend on operator-defined economic parameters and priorities. Embedding a Large Language Model (LLM) directly in a recurring control loop, by contrast, may introduce non-negligible inference latency and non-deterministic behavior. This paper proposes a two-timescale architecture that addresses this gap: an LLM-based Strategist translates natural-language network intents into economic policy parameters (budget, income, and bidding-aggressiveness) at the macro scale, while a deterministic, Vickrey-inspired second-price mechanism run by SPADE-based slice agents resolves recurring resource contention at the operational scale, with the resulting allocations enforced by patching Kubernetes CPU limits and Calico CNI bandwidth annotations directly on User Plane Function pods. We evaluate the architecture end-to-end on a real Kubernetes/Open5GS edge cluster across two complementary scenarios, each repeated 30 times: a Smart-City scenario, in which natural-language public-safety intents propagate into measurable changes in slice bids and bandwidth allocations within a few auction rounds, and a Hierarchical-slicing scenario, in which the mechanism provides priority-dependent resource and QoE differentiation, adapts to abrupt demand changes, and preserves configured minimum guarantees while maintaining near-complete allocation of the shared bandwidth pool.

</details>


### [8] [Clock-Free Stochastic Scheduling for Real-Time Synchronized Content Delivery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.15114)
*Xuan Wang,Xiaoyi Wu,Ying Sun,Atilla Eryilmaz,Bin Li*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 提出一种无时钟同步的调度框架，通过延迟补偿控制服务器对每个用户的发送时间，在低延迟代价下最大化同步性能；将问题建模为凸随机优化，采用SPP与ADMM求解，复杂度O(N log N)，收敛率O(1/√K)，并扩展至丢包场景。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 云游戏、协作混合现实与直播等实时同步内容分发场景中，服务器需保证一组用户同时感知相同内容，但网络延迟异构且不可预测，同步极具挑战。既有工作依赖同步时钟或客户端时间戳，部署受限。

Method: 提出无时钟调度框架，引入延迟补偿控制服务器对每个用户的发送时刻，仅使用ACK反馈；将问题形式化为带非光滑目标的凸随机优化，采用随机近端点（SPP）算法仅依赖历史延迟样本更新补偿量；利用子问题结构结合ADMM实现高效更新，每次迭代复杂度至多O(N log N)。

Result: 建立O(1/√K)的收敛率（K为内容传输次数）；框架可扩展处理传输失败，即丢包仅使部分用户成功接收；在合成与真实延迟轨迹上的仿真表明算法一致优于基线方法。

Conclusion: 该无时钟、基于ACK反馈的延迟补偿框架在无需时钟同步的前提下兼顾同步性能与低延迟代价，具备高效计算与理论收敛保证，并能应对丢包，适合多样化环境部署。

Abstract: Real-time synchronized content delivery is critical for applications such as cloud gaming, collaborative mixed reality, and live streaming, where a server must ensure that a group of users perceives the same content simultaneously for each delivery. Due to heterogeneous and unpredictable network delays, ensuring synchronization is highly challenging. We propose a clock-free scheduling framework that introduces latency compensation to control the server's delivery time for each user, aiming to maximize synchronization performance (i.e., minimizing the gap between the maximum and minimum latencies) while maintaining a low overall latency cost. Unlike prior work requiring synchronized clocks or client-side timestamps, our approach uses only acknowledgment feedback, enabling flexible deployment across diverse environments. We formulate the problem as a convex stochastic optimization with a non-smooth objective. To solve the problem, we employ the stochastic proximal point (SPP) algorithm that updates the delay compensation, using only the previous latency samples, rather than the latency distribution. By exploiting the subproblem structure, we apply ADMM to enable efficient updates with per-iteration complexity of at most $\mathcal{O}(N\log N)$, where $N$ is the number of users. We establish a convergence rate of $\mathcal{O}(1/\sqrt{K})$, where $K$ is the number of content deliveries for transmission. The framework is further extended to handle transmission failures, where packet loss results in successful delivery to only a subset of users. Finally, simulations using both synthetic and real-world latency traces demonstrate the consistent superiority of our algorithm against baseline methods.

</details>


### [9] [Tutorial on Closed-Loop and Application-Aware Digital Twins for Future Wireless Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.15120)
*Yuchen Liu,Sara Garcia Sanchez,Mingzhe Chen,Xingqin Lin,Berk Canberk,Tommaso Melodia*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 该教程综述了数字孪生网络（DTN）的设计与优化，涵盖孪生建模、资源与语义优化、物理层优化、闭环反馈管理及标准化工具，旨在为面向应用感知、AI原生、闭环无线系统提供统一视角。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为支撑扩展现实、自主系统和大规模信息物理基础设施等新兴应用，下一代无线网络需超越传统通信范式，转向紧耦合的闭环系统。数字孪生网络通过实时双向交互与自适应控制连接物理与数字世界，成为该转型的关键使能技术，因此需要对其设计与优化进行系统性梳理。

Method: 采用教程式综述方法，从多个系统维度组织内容：（1）DTN构建的基本设计原则，包括高保真孪生建模、遥测集成与信息物理同步；（2）资源优化与语义驱动DTN，以应用感知目标和语义抽象引导数据处理、通信与决策；（3）DTN物理层优化，强调联合感知、通信与自适应无线资源控制；（4）DTN引导的网络管理与反馈优化，聚焦闭环学习、实时控制与强化学习驱动的自适应；（5）标准化进展与使能工具。

Result: 形成了覆盖DTN构建、资源与语义优化、物理层优化、闭环网络管理以及标准化与工具的综合性技术框架，并指出DTN是面向应用感知、AI原生与闭环无线系统的新兴范式，同时总结了关键挑战与未来研究方向。

Conclusion: DTN作为连接物理与数字世界的关键范式，需要在建模保真度、语义与资源协同、物理层联合优化以及闭环学习控制等方面协同推进；其实际部署还依赖标准化与工具支持，未来研究应围绕这些方向展开。

Abstract: To support emerging applications such as extended reality, autonomous systems, and large-scale cyber-physical infrastructures, next-generation wireless networks must evolve beyond traditional communication paradigms toward tightly integrated and closed-loop systems. Digital Twin Networks (DTNs) have recently emerged as a key enabler of this transformation by bridging the physical and digital worlds through real-time, bidirectional interactions and adaptive control mechanisms. In this tutorial, we provide a comprehensive overview of DTN design and optimization across multiple system dimensions. Specifically, we first introduce the fundamental design principles of DTN creation, including high-fidelity twin modeling, telemetry integration, and cyber-physical synchronization. We then present resource optimization and semantic-driven DTNs, where application-aware objectives and semantic abstractions guide efficient data processing, communication, and decision-making. Subsequently, we explore physical-layer optimization techniques for DTNs, emphasizing joint sensing, communication, and adaptive wireless resource control. Furthermore, we examine DTN-guided network management and feedback optimization, focusing on closed-loop learning, real-time control, and reinforcement-driven adaptation between digital twins and physical networks. Finally, we review ongoing standardization efforts and enabling tools that support the practical deployment of DTNs in real-world systems. This tutorial aims to provide a unified perspective on DTNs as an emerging paradigm for application-aware, AI-native, and closed-loop wireless systems, and to highlight key challenges and future research directions in this rapidly evolving field.

</details>


### [10] [NAS Timers for Non-Terrestrial Networks: Current Standards and Directions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.15211)
*Cheng Liu,Peng Hu*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 本文综述5G NAS响应监督定时器在非地面网络中的语义与延迟构成，沿配置粒度与信息范围两个维度梳理候选改进方案，并通过注册案例说明定时器配置的权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在非地面网络（NTN）中，5G NAS响应监督定时器覆盖的时延区间可能包含卫星传播、地面传输及多个核心网功能的处理时间。现行3GPP规范依据卫星NG-RAN无线接入技术（RAT）类型选取定时器值，但同一低地球轨道（LEO）网络内的控制路径在传播时延与处理负载上可能存在差异，因此现有配置方式可能不够精细。

Method: 文章回顾相关3GPP定时器语义，沿5G注册流程追踪不同定时器所覆盖的各延迟分量；并沿配置粒度（configuration granularity）与信息范围（information scope）两个维度组织候选改进方案，结合注册案例进行分析。

Result: 注册案例研究展示了在避免定时器过早超时与在消息丢失后及时恢复之间的权衡关系。

Conclusion: 文章识别出未来规范为实现可互操作的定时器配置所需解决的信息与协调问题。

Abstract: Response-supervision timers in the 5G Non-Access Stratum (NAS) bound the time allowed for signaling exchanges before retransmission or abnormal-case handling begins. In non-terrestrial networks, that interval can include satellite propagation, terrestrial transport, and processing by multiple core-network functions. Under current 3GPP specifications, the applicable timer value for satellite access is selected according to the satellite NG-RAN radio access technology (RAT) type. However, control paths within the same low Earth orbit (LEO) network can differ in propagation delay and processing load. This article reviews the relevant 3GPP timer semantics and traces the delay components covered by different timers through 5G registration. It organizes candidate refinements along two dimensions: configuration granularity and information scope. A registration case study illustrates the trade-off between avoiding premature timer expiry and recovering promptly from message loss. The article identifies the information and coordination that future specifications would need to address to support interoperable timer configuration.

</details>


### [11] [Cost-Aware Conflict Triage for xApp Control Conflicts in Open RAN](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.15310)
*Abdul Wadud,Fatemeh Golpayegani,Nima Afraz*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 提出成本感知的冲突分诊框架，将Open RAN中xApp冲突检测从"严重度评估"推进到"事件级成本-收益决策"，实现容忍/延迟/缓解三分类处置。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Open RAN支持多厂商并发部署，多个xApp在Near-RT RIC中独立优化移动性、负载、覆盖与能耗；当控制参数重叠时，孤立控制环路产生冲突决策并恶化网络KPI。既有冲突评估方法只能估计严重度，无法回答"该干预是否成本合理"，导致低价值干预泛滥。

Method: 1) 基于ECDF的严重度评估；2) 将检测到的冲突记录聚合为episode（事件级单元）；3) 提取9维特征；4) 估计各机制的干预成本与收益；5) 在事件级决策层输出三选一动作：tolerate（容忍）、defer（延迟）、mitigate（缓解）。评估环境为仿真驱动的Open RAN场景，4个并发xApp，30次蒙特卡洛运行。

Result: 框架对检测到的episode处置比例为：defer 66.8%、mitigate 19.8%、tolerate 13.4%；平均每次干预净效用0.132，较"始终缓解"（always-mitigate）基线提升3.9倍，收益主要来自通过成本感知的选择性规避低价值干预。

Conclusion: 成本感知选择性能有效筛除低价值冲突干预，将冲突管理目标由单纯降severity转向最大化净效用，为Open RAN多xApp冲突治理提供可落地的三分类分诊范式。

Abstract: Open Radio Access Network (Open RAN) enables multi-vendor deployments where concurrent RAN control applications (xApps) independently optimise mobility, load, coverage, and energy within the Near Real Time RAN Intelligent Controller (Near-RT RIC). When xApps act on overlapping control parameters, their isolated loops produce conflicting decisions that degrade the network Key Performance Indicators (KPIs). Existing conflict-evaluation methods estimate severity but do not answer whether the intervention is cost-justified. This paper proposes a cost-aware conflict triage framework that pairs ECDF-based severity evaluation with an event-level decision layer. The framework groups detected conflict records into episodes, extracts nine features, estimates per-mechanism costs and benefits, and assigns one of three actions: tolerate, defer, or mitigate. Evaluated in a simulator-driven Open RAN setting over 30 Monte Carlo runs with four concurrent xApps, the framework defers 66.8 %, mitigates 19.8 %, and tolerates 13.4 % of detected episodes, achieving a mean net utility of 0.132 per intervention, 3.9x higher than an always-mitigate baseline, by avoiding low- value interventions through cost-aware selectivity.

</details>


### [12] [The Evolution of In-Premise Networks In the Era of Immersive Services: From Copper to Fiber-to-The Room [Invited Tutorial]](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.15566)
*Elaine Wong,Sourav Mondal*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 本文分析体验驱动时代协作式扩展现实（XR）对网络的严苛需求，指出接入网与室内网络间的性能差距推动光纤进一步入户，提出融合粗粒度流量预测与室内智能漫游的资源分配框架，并在FTTH/FTTR/WiFi多层级网络中验证其可满足协作XR的QoE要求，同时探讨摊薄FTTR资本开支的策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 电信业正转向由沉浸式应用定义的体验驱动时代，此类应用对网络提出严苛要求。虽然基于PON的FTTH架构历史上提供了高容量宽带，但接入网与室内网络之间日益扩大的性能差距，使得必须将光纤进一步深入用户驻地。为补充现有铜缆与WiFi技术，FTTR成为关键使能技术。文章聚焦协作式扩展现实（XR）这一典型驱动力，需要针对其多模态流量特征与QoE需求提出新的网络架构与资源管理方案。

Method: 文章首先分析协作式XR的多模态业务流量特征与体验质量（QoE）需求，以及由此带来的网络性能影响；随后提出一种新型资源分配框架，将粗粒度流量预测与室内智能漫游相结合，并支持依据FTTH、FTTR与WiFi各段计算能力灵活部署定制化人工智能模型；最后对代表性多层级网络进行数值评估。

Result: 数值评估表明，长时域、多步前向的流量预测可显著降低推理频率；该方案能够实现协同资源分配与跨房间的网络编排无缝移动性，有效满足协作式XR的QoE要求。此外，文章提出通过将光基础设施的作用从基础连接扩展到多业务统一物理层承载，可提高基础设施利用率、开辟新收入来源并最大化室内光纤部署价值，从而摊薄FTTR资本开支。

Conclusion: FTTR是弥合接入网与室内网络性能差距、支撑协作式XR等沉浸式应用的关键使能技术；融合粗粒度流量预测与智能漫游的资源分配框架可有效满足XR的QoE需求；通过统一物理层承载多样化业务可提升FTTR基础设施利用效率并摊薄资本开支，实现商业价值最大化。

Abstract: The telecommunications industry is shifting toward an experience-driven era defined by immersive applications that impose stringent network demands. While passive optical network (PON)-based Fiber-To-The-Home (FTTH) architectures have historically delivered high-capacity broadband, a growing performance gap between access and indoor networks necessitates driving fiber deeper into premises. Complementing existing copper and WiFi technologies, Fiber-to-the-Room (FTTR) is emerging as a critical enabler. This article examines the drivers of this architectural shift, specifically focusing on collaborative extended reality (XR). We analyze the multi-modal traffic characteristics and Quality-of-Experience (QoE) requirements of XR, alongside the resulting network performance implications. To address these demands, we propose a novel resource allocation framework integrating coarse-grained traffic forecasting with intelligent in-premise roaming. This framework facilitates the flexible deployment of artificial intelligence models tailored to the computational capabilities of the FTTH, FTTR, and WiFi segments. Numerical evaluations of a representative multi-tier network demonstrate that long-horizon, multi-step-ahead traffic forecasts significantly reduce inference frequency. This approach enables coordinated resource allocation and network-orchestrated seamless mobility across rooms, effectively satisfying collaborative XR QoE requirements. Finally, we highlight strategies to amortize FTTR capital expenditure by extending the optical infrastructure's role beyond basic connectivity. By supporting diverse services over a unified physical layer, FTTR enhances infrastructure utilization, unlocks novel revenue streams, and maximizes the value of in-premise fiber deployments.

</details>


### [13] [Coarse-Grained Predictive Resource Allocation Framework enabling In-Premise XR Collaborations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.15581)
*Sourav Mondal,Elaine Wong*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 本文提出一种面向沉浸式XR协作的分层AI资源分配与室内漫游框架：在FTTR段用TCN-BiLSTM做多模态流量预测，在FTTH段用Transformer类时序模型预测汇聚流量，实现跨FTTH-FTTR-WiFi的主动资源分配，仿真显示显著优于传统方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 沉浸式XR是下一代网络人本连接的关键使能技术，但其高吞吐、超低时延、高可靠需求与防晕动症要求，在无线连接非确定的环境下难以满足。FTTR可扩展FTTH基础设施，但XR协作者严格的QoE需求要求跨FTTH、FTTR、WiFi多级级联的鲁棒资源分配。

Method: 提出粗粒度流量预测赋能的主动资源分配与智能室内漫游框架，AI模型复杂度按各网段算力分级适配。FTTR段采用混合TCN-BiLSTM网络进行多模态XR流量预测，集成PON与支持MLO和OFDMA的WiFi接入点；长时程预测降低推理频率，实现FTTR-WiFi协同资源分配与网络编排的跨房间无缝移动。FTTH段部署Transformer类时序模型（Informer、Autoformer、PatchTST、TFT）预测FTTR段聚合的复杂XR流量以用于回传。

Result: 评估表明，Transformer类模型在长时程预测上比混合模型精度最高提升约75%；大量仿真证实所提框架在满足XR QoE需求方面显著优于传统方法。

Conclusion: 分级AI预测与主动资源分配相结合，可有效支撑FTTH-FTTR-WiFi级联网络下的XR QoE保障；Transformer类模型更适合FTTH段的汇聚流量长时程预测。

Abstract: Immersive extended reality (XR) applications are key enablers of human-centric connectivity in next-generation networks. However, meeting their high-throughput, ultra-low-latency, and high-reliability demands while preventing cyber-sickness remains a critical challenge, especially in environments with non-deterministic wireless connectivity. Fiber-To-The-Room (FTTR), extending widely deployed Fiber-To-The-Home (FTTH) infrastructure, offers a promising solution. Yet, satisfying the stringent Quality-of-Experience (QoE) requirements of XR collaborators demands robust resource allocation across the multi-tier cascade of FTTH, FTTR, and WiFi segments. In this paper, we propose a coarse-grained traffic forecasting-empowered proactive resource allocation and intelligent in-premise roaming framework. Here, artificial intelligence (AI) model complexities are scaled to the computational capabilities of each network segment. At the FTTR segment, we employ a hybrid temporal convolutional and bi-directional long short-term memory (TCN-BiLSTM) network for multi-modal XR traffic forecasting, integrating passive optical network (PON) and WiFi access points with multi-link operation (MLO) and orthogonal frequency-division multiple access (OFDMA). The resulting long-horizon forecasts reduce inference frequency, enabling coordinated FTTR-WiFi resource allocation and seamless network-orchestrated mobility across rooms. At the FTTH segment, we deploy Transformer-based time-series models (e.g., Informer, Autoformer, PatchTST, and TFT) to predict the complex XR traffic aggregated in FTTR segments for transmission over the FTTH backhaul. Evaluations show Transformer-based models are up to $\sim$75% more accurate in long-horizon forecasting than the hybrid model. Extensive simulations confirm the proposed framework significantly outperforms traditional methods in satisfying XR QoE requirements.

</details>


### [14] [NetRL: Powering Network Decisions via NetGRPO-Driven Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.16226)
*Lingfeng Peng,Xiaohui Xie,Yang Bao,Yong Cui*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 提出NetRL——基于LLM的两阶段强化学习框架，统一处理多种网络决策任务，在QoE、任务完成时间与网络效用上均达到当前最优。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 网络决策任务（自适应码率、负载均衡、拥塞控制）需要策略处理异构状态与动作，并权衡短期与长期后果。现有方法跨任务泛化差、需大量任务特定改造、缺乏对长时程动作后果的可靠评估，且在线交互学习面临优化不稳定问题。

Method: 两阶段LLM强化学习：先对示教轨迹做监督微调，再进行在线策略优化。采用通用混合嵌入接口，将文本任务描述与精确数值状态结合；网络动作头输出合法决策。核心是NetGRPO：在多条未来轨迹上评估候选动作，通过置信度感知预算将更深搜索导向不确定的备选动作，并执行组相对、裁剪及KL正则化的更新。

Result: 相比NetLLM的QoE提升23.6%，相比Genet的任务完成时间降低12.2%，相比Orca的网络效用提升24.1%，在评估方法中达到最优。置信度感知预算在不降低验证QoE的情况下将端到端训练时间减少27.1%。方法收敛稳定，且无需重训练即可泛化到未见网络轨迹。

Conclusion: NetRL通过LLM与强化学习的结合及置信度感知搜索机制，实现了跨网络任务的统一、稳定且高效的学习，具备良好的泛化能力，代码与模型权重已开源。

Abstract: Network decision-making tasks, including adaptive bitrate streaming, load balancing, and congestion control, require
  policies to process heterogeneous states and actions while balancing near- and long-term consequences. Existing solutions
  generalize poorly across network tasks, require substantial task-specific redesign, and lack reliable evaluation of long-
  horizon action consequences. Methods that learn through online interaction additionally face unstable optimization. We
  present NetRL, a two-stage LLM-based reinforcement learning framework that combines supervised fine-tuning on
  demonstration trajectories with online policy optimization. A universal mixed-embedding interface combines textual task
  descriptions with precise numerical states, while a network action head produces valid decisions. At its core, NetGRPO
  evaluates candidate actions over multiple future trajectories, directs deeper search toward uncertain alternatives
  through confidence-aware budgeting, and performs group-relative, clipped, and KL-regularized updates. NetRL achieves
  23.6% higher QoE than NetLLM, 12.2% lower job completion time than Genet, and 24.1% higher network utility than Orca,
  yielding state-of-the-art performance among the evaluated methods. Confidence-aware budgeting reduces end-to-end training
  time by 27.1% without reducing validation QoE. NetRL also converges stably and generalizes to unseen network traces
  without retraining. Code and model weights are available at the repository linked in this paper.

</details>


### [15] [Island-Ready Communication Networks: Connectivity Monitoring and State Synchronization for 5G Crisis Connectivity at the Edge](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.16284)
*Leon Janzen,Maria Koch,Matthias Hollick*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 本文面向5G孤岛运行（island-ready）需求，研究状态同步与连接监控两大功能，基于开源项目集成方案并在全国范围5G仿真网络中评估切换时延与同步开销。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 孤立地区在危机场景下与全球互联网断开时，需通过切换至孤岛运行维持边缘连接。然而孤岛就绪的实际落地受制于状态同步与连接监控等未解研究挑战，本文旨在攻克这些阻碍其实用化的开放问题。

Method: 将所提解决方案集成到开源项目中，构建全国范围的5G通信网络仿真环境并注入真实蜂窝流量，作为概念验证与性能评估手段；重点考察向孤岛运行切换的时延构成，以及高度分布式核心网的状态同步开销随5G核心数量的增长规律。

Result: 切换时延评估表明，大部分切换延迟源自接入网重配置；同步开销评估显示，同步流量随5G核心数量呈超线性增长，在50个核心的部署下同步数据速率峰值达9.36MB/s。

Conclusion: 孤岛就绪5G的实用化关键在于接入网重配置优化与分布式核心网同步开销控制；本文的开源集成与仿真验证为孤岛运行的状态同步和连接监控设计提供了可量化的性能依据。

Abstract: Island-ready 5G deployments provide edge connectivity for isolated regions in crisis scenarios by switching to island operation when they are cut off from the global Internet. This work addresses open research challenges that hinder the practical realization of island readiness, with a focus on the required state synchronization and connectivity monitoring functionality. As a proof of concept and for performance evaluation, we integrate our solutions in open-source projects and emulate a country-wide 5G communication network with real-world cellular traffic. Our evaluation studies the switching latency of transitions to island operation, showing that most of the transition delay stems from the access network reconfiguration. We study the synchronization overhead of a highly distributed core network, finding that the synchronization traffic grows faster than linearly with the number of 5G cores, reaching peak synchronization data rates of 9.36MB/s for a deployment of 50 cores.

</details>


### [16] [CALIPER: Calibrating HPC/AI Network Simulators](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.16477)
*Seyyidahmed Lahmer,Daniel Seibel,Srijeet Basu,Arno Troch,Jayesh Badwaik,Subhomoy Bhattacharyya,Abubakr Nada,Debjyoti Bhattacharjee,Vinay Kumar Baapanapalli Yadaiah,Michael Peeters,Andreas Herten,Darayus Adil Patel*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 提出CALIPER——一套面向HPC/AI网络模拟器的系统化校准与验证方法论，通过参数分类、分层尺度感知校准与约束推断，在64节点参考系统上实现跨域预测误差低于5%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 网络模拟是HPC/AI系统研究的重要手段，可用于探索在实验中难以实现的网络架构、通信策略与系统配置规模。但现有模拟器校准与验证方法缺乏系统性方法学，未能将参数对齐、约束推断与独立验证结合，尤其缺少面向集合通信（collective-centric）网络的原则性流程。

Method: CALIPER将系统参数区分为可直接观测参数与需通过校准确定的辅助参数，并将后者约束在与目标系统一致的可行域内；采用分层、尺度感知（scale-aware）的校准流程，结合受限推断，并考察参数可辨识性与泛化能力。评估基于德国于利希超算中心的参考系统，使用集合通信基准，规模至多64节点。

Result: 经CALIPER校准得到的参数配置，在scale-up与scale-out两类网络域上预测系统性能的平均误差均低于5%；分层约束校准提升了参数可辨识性，并使预测精度可延续到拟合所用观测之外。

Conclusion: 分层、受约束的校准能够产生具有泛化预测能力的模拟器配置，为HPC/AI网络模拟器的校准与验证提供了可复现的方法学范式。

Abstract: Network simulation plays an important role in HPC/AI system research by enabling exploration of network architectures, communication strategies, and system configurations at scales that may be impractical to study experimentally. Existing approaches to network simulator calibration and validation lack a principled methodology that combines parameter alignment, constrained inference, and independent validation for collective-centric HPC/AI networks. We present CALIPER, a methodology for systematic calibration and validation of HPC/AI network simulators. CALIPER distinguishes directly observable system parameters from auxiliary parameters which are configured via calibration and constrains the latter to feasible domains consistent with the target system. We evaluate CALIPER's hierarchical, scale-aware calibration and its effects on parameter identifiability and generalization using collective communication benchmarks on a reference system with up to 64-nodes at the Jülich Supercomputing Centre. Validation results show that the calibrated parameter configurations identified by CALIPER predict system performance with less than 5% mean errors across scale-up and scale-out network domains. CALIPER demonstrates that hierarchical, constrained calibration can produce simulator configurations whose predictive accuracy persists beyond the observations used for fitting.

</details>


### [17] [Offline-Tuned Service-Aware TN-NTN Feeder Allocation for 6G Post-Disaster Service Resilience](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.16501)
*Donglin Wang,Anjie Qiu,Qiuheng Zhou,Hans D. Schotten*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 本文研究灾后TN失效下，通过服务优先级感知的NTN馈线容量编排提升网络韧性；用离线CEM调优馈线权重等参数，仿真显示在严重故障时可显著提升应急加权连续性指标，但轻微损伤可能反而降低聚合连续性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 灾害导致地面网络（TN）失效，幸存基站过载并中断应急服务；非地面网络（NTN）可提供回退，但用户迁移会使馈线链路成为瓶颈，因此需要研究受限NTN馈线容量下的灾后网络韧性编排。

Method: 将问题建模为后故障快照下服务优先级感知的NTN馈线容量编排；采用轻量级交叉熵方法（CEM）离线调优馈线、卸载与用户排序参数，而非在线AI控制器；并使用Python系统级仿真器，在抽象TN-NTN链路与排队模型下扫描运行区域。

Result: 增益依赖故障严重程度。在总TN中断、180-Mbps馈线下，所提策略将绝对应急加权连续性指标Cemg从0.399提升至0.666（相较匹配规则基线且均匀馈线缩放；配对增益0.267，95% bootstrap CI [0.264, 0.271]）；轻微损伤可能降低聚合连续性。离线CEM馈线权重调优在严重故障下比固定服务优先级进一步改善连续性，而调优卸载与用户排序未带来可检测的增量增益。

Conclusion: 在灾后TN失效场景中，服务优先级感知的NTN馈线容量编排可显著提升严重故障下的应急连续性；其主要增益来自离线CEM对馈线权重的调优，而卸载与用户排序的调优增益有限，且轻微损伤下可能不利。

Abstract: Terrestrial network (TN) failures during disasters can overload surviving base stations (BSs) and disrupt emergency services. A non-terrestrial network (NTN) provides fallback, but its feeder link can become a bottleneck as users migrate. This paper studies post-disaster network resilience through service-priority-aware orchestration of constrained NTN feeder capacity in a post-failure snapshot. A lightweight Cross-Entropy Method (CEM) tunes feeder, offload, and user-ranking parameters offline, not as an online artificial intelligence (AI) controller. A Python system-level simulator sweeps operating regions under abstract TN-NTN link and queueing models. Gains depend on failure severity. At a 180-Mbps feeder under total TN outage, the proposed policy increases the absolute emergency-weighted continuity metric Cemg from 0.399 to 0.666 relative to the matched rule baseline with uniform feeder scaling (paired gain: 0.267; 95% bootstrap CI: [0.264, 0.271]); mild damage can reduce aggregate continuity. Offline CEM feeder-weight tuning further improves severe-failure continuity over fixed service priorities, whereas tuned offload and user ranking provide no detectable incremental gain.

</details>


### [18] [MCCL: Measurement-based Collective Communication Library](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.17091)
*Michele Ferrero,Gabriele Castellano,Paolo Medagliani,Sebastien Martin,Roberto Morabito,Massimo Gallo*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: MCCL通过闭环的运行时网络测量与动态调度合成，解决了分布式训练中因灰色故障导致的预计算调度效率下降问题，在灰色故障下最高减少训练时间4.99倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在规模化分布式训练中，集合通信是关键性能瓶颈。现有库与合成框架采用开环方式一次性计算调度，假设拓扑静态、链路容量处于峰值。但在生产环境中，持续性灰色故障（gray failures）会悄然降低链路吞吐与延迟并持续数小时，使预计算调度在执行中大幅失效。

Method: 提出MCCL，一个将运行时网络测量与动态调度合成闭环结合的集合通信库。它被动测量GPU到GPU吞吐，检测持续性性能退化，使用热启动（warm-started）合成器对受影响的通信密集型调度重新合成，并在不中断训练的前提下热切换调度。作者在多节点硬件原型上验证完整闭环，并用仿真评估集群规模场景。

Result: MCCL开销可忽略（<2%），测量精度高（误差<1.2%），调度热切换时间低（约12 ms）。在灰色故障下，针对Llama、DeepSeek等先进LLM的模拟训练负载，相比静态调度最多将训练时间减少4.99倍。

Conclusion: 通过将运行时测量与动态调度合成闭环，MCCL能够在不中断训练的情况下适应持续性链路退化，显著提升灰色故障场景下的分布式训练效率，且额外开销极低。

Abstract: Collective communication is a critical performance bottleneck in large-scale distributed training. Existing libraries and synthesis frameworks compute open-loop schedules once, assuming a static topology and peak link capacities. In production, however, persistent gray failures can silently degrade link throughput and latency for hours, causing pre-computed schedules to lose much of their efficiency during execution. In this paper, we present Measurement-based Collective Communication Library (MCCL), a collective communication library that closes the loop between runtime network measurement and dynamic schedule synthesis. MCCL passively measures GPU-to-GPU throughput, detects persistent degradation, re-synthesizes the affected communication-intensive schedules with warm-started synthesizers, and hot-swaps them without interrupting training. We validate the complete loop on a multi-node hardware prototype and use simulations to evaluate cluster-scale scenarios. In our experiments, MCCL incurs negligible overhead (<2%) while maintaining high measurement accuracy (<1.2% error) and low schedule hot-swap time (about 12 ms). Notably, under gray failures, MCCL reduces training time by up to 4.99x over static schedules on simulated training workloads for state-of-the-art LLMs such as Llama and DeepSeek.

</details>


### [19] [Computing All-Pairs Shortest Communication Time Paths for Periodically Varying Satellite Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.17372)
*Koushik Sinha,Dibakar Saha,Sai Puppala,Bhabani P. Sinha*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 本文针对时变网络（LEO卫星星座）提出一种全对最短时间路径算法，时间复杂度O(M²n³)，其计算结构类似两次矩阵乘法（前向+后向），适合GPU等SIMD架构并行加速，可实现快速变化星座的高效全网路由。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LEO卫星星座的拓扑随时间快速变化，传统静态图上的最短路径算法难以高效处理这类动态时变网络，而网络范围内的消息路由又要求快速计算全对最短时间路径，因此需要一个兼顾效率与可并行性的算法。

Method: 将时变网络建模为时序图 G=(V, E_t)，其中 V 为 n 个顶点，E_t 为在总时间窗 T=Mτ（τ 为每个时间区间时长）内的 M 条时变边。算法在 O(M²n³) 时间内求解全对最短路径，其计算结构对应前向与后向两个矩阵乘法步骤，从而适配 SIMD/GPU 并行架构。

Result: 算法在 O(M²n³) 时间内求得全对最短时间路径；由于其双层矩阵乘法式结构，可高效并行化到 GPU 等 SIMD 架构，相比传统最短路径算法取得显著加速，并支持快速变化的 LEO 卫星星座的全网消息路由。

Conclusion: 该工作表明，面向时序图的矩阵乘法式算法结构能够有效利用并行硬件，为动态 LEO 卫星星座提供高效的全对最短路径计算与全网路由能力。

Abstract: We address the problem of designing an efficient algorithm for computing the all-pairs shortest-time paths in time-varying networks, specifically targeting the dynamic topological characteristics of low earth orbit (LEO) satellite constellations. We model such temporal graphs as $G = (V, E_t)$, where $V=\{1, 2,\ldots , n\}$ is the set of $n$ vertices in the graph, and $E_t$ is the set of $M$ time-varying edges considered over a total time window of size $T = Mτ$, $τ$ being the duration of each interval in that time window. For such a network, our algorithm finds the all-pair shortest paths in $O(M^2n^3)$-time. A key advantage of our approach is its computational structure, which mirrors two matrix multiplication steps, first in the forward direction and then in the backward direction. This structure of the algorithm makes it exceptionally suitable for parallelization on SIMD architectures like GPUs, thereby offering a significant speedup over traditional shortest-path algorithms and enabling efficient, network-wide message routing in rapidly changing LEO satellite constellations.

</details>


### [20] [What Is the Cost of a Handover Interruption? Rethinking 5G Mobility for Aerial Vehicles](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.17380)
*André Melo,Yueqi Chen,Igor Kadota,Lourenço Alves Pereira*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 论文提出风险感知移动性（RAM）方法，将5G预测的通信中断映射为运营风险代理指标，在圣保罗188个授权5G扇区的密集部署中，风险感知切换时机使平均运营风险代理降低45%，且不损害服务链路质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 低空航空正走向大规模运营，日益依赖5G蜂窝连接。但5G移动性对其决策的运营风险是"盲"的：同一次中断在空旷田野巡航中无害，在障碍物、人群或其他飞行器附近却可能致命。5G虽已能获取位置、人口密度和无线条件，但移动性决策未将这些信息转化为运营风险度量。

Method: 提出风险感知移动性（RAM）：利用信息陈旧度、位置不确定性和运营上下文，将预测的通信中断映射为运营风险代理指标，并将该风险暴露给现有移动性机制（如基于风险感知的切换时机决策）。

Result: 在圣保罗188个授权5G扇区的密集部署中，风险感知切换时机相比传统A3事件，平均运营风险代理降低45%，且不降低服务链路质量。

Conclusion: 结果推动5G移动性从优化切换性能转向管理移动性决策的运营风险。除切换时机外，还引出更广泛的问题：当以运营风险而非单纯无线性能评估连接性时，哪些小区应服务于空中运营。

Abstract: Low-altitude aviation is moving toward large-scale operations increasingly dependent on 5G cellular connectivity. However, 5G mobility remains blind to the operational risk of its decisions: the same interruption may be harmless in cruise over an empty field but critical near obstacles, crowds or other aircraft. Although 5G already provides mechanisms to obtain location, population density, and radio conditions, mobility decisions do not translate this information into an operational risk metric. We propose Risk-Aware Mobility (RAM), which maps predicted communication disruptions to an operational-risk proxy using information staleness, position uncertainty, and operational context, and exposes this risk to existing mobility mechanisms. In a dense São Paulo deployment with 188 licensed 5G sectors, risk-aware handover timing reduces the mean operational-risk proxy by 45\% relative to conventional A3 without degrading serving-link quality. These results motivate shifting 5G mobility from optimizing handover performance to managing the operational risk of mobility decisions. Beyond handover timing, this perspective raises a broader question: which cells should serve aerial operations when connectivity is evaluated by operational risk rather than radio performance alone?

</details>


### [21] [QoE-Aware Service Function Chain Orchestration in Space-Air-Ground Integrated Networks for In-Flight Internet](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.17851)
*Feng Liu,Bing Du,Ziyang Guo,Xiaoqi Zhang,Haijun Zhang*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 提出融合地面网络、民航网络与LEO卫星的SAGIN架构，基于真实北大西洋航班数据构建异构航空互联网，以复合QoE指标（时延、抖动、内容相似度）为导向，将数据交付建模为RTEG上的SFC部署长期QoE最大化问题，并设计融合LCSS路径过滤与softmax动作选择的MADDPG算法求解，实现异构飞机无缝连接并优于博弈论与MATD3方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 偏远地区缺乏地面基础设施，而航空用户对互联网服务的需求已日益接近地面蜂窝网络水平，现有单一网络难以满足异构飞机多样化的服务需求，因而需要空地一体化协同网络与高效的资源调度机制。

Method: 1) 构建包含地面网络、民航网络与LEO卫星的SAGIN架构，基于真实北大西洋航班数据生成异构航空互联网；2) 设计以传输时延、抖动和内容相似度为表征的复合QoE指标；3) 采用可重构时间扩展图（RTEG）刻画SAGIN的动态连通性；4) 借助NFV将数据交付形式化为跨地面站、中继飞机与卫星的服务功能链（SFC）部署，形成长期QoE最大化问题；5) 提出定制化MADDPG算法，融入基于LCSS的路径过滤与基于softmax的动作选择。

Result: 仿真结果表明，所提方法能够为异构飞机提供无缝连接，并在QoE性能上持续优于博弈论方法与MATD3方法。

Conclusion: SAGIN与SFC部署相结合的建模方式以及定制化MADDPG求解策略，可有效提升航空互联网的长期QoE与异构飞机连接连续性，验证了空地一体化资源优化框架的可行性。

Abstract: In remote regions lacking terrestrial infrastructure, aviation users increasingly demand Internet services comparable to ground cellular networks. This paper proposes a space-air-ground integrated network (SAGIN) architecture that combines ground networks, civil aviation networks, and low Earth orbit (LEO) satellites. Based on real North Atlantic flight data, a heterogeneous aviation Internet is constructed to support diverse aircraft service requirements.
  A composite Quality of Experience (QoE) metric, characterized by transmission delay, jitter, and content similarity, guides efficient resource utilization. The dynamic connectivity of SAGIN is modeled using a reconfigurable time-expanded graph (RTEG). With Network Function Virtualization (NFV), data delivery is formulated as a Service Function Chain (SFC) deployment across ground stations, relay aircraft, and satellites, resulting in a long-term QoE maximization problem.
  A tailored Multi-Agent Deep Deterministic Policy Gradient (MADDPG) algorithm incorporating LCSS-based path filtering and softmax-based action selection is developed. Simulation results demonstrate that the proposed approach enables seamless connectivity for heterogeneous aircraft and consistently outperforms game-theoretic and MATD3 methods in terms of QoE.

</details>


### [22] [Towards Virtual Networking for a Time-Triggered Hypervisor](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.17899)
*Jan Ruh,Silviu S. Craciunas,Jan Korecky*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 提出基于VirtIO的虚拟网络，实现IEEE 802.1Qbv准兼容的虚拟机间通信，为时间触发虚拟机提供可预测的端到端时延。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 虚拟化让混合关键性应用通过隔离在独立虚拟机中共享硬件，但在不依赖静态分区的情况下，为VCPU上的关键任务提供端到端时序保证很难。一种思路是采用时间触发hypervisor按预定全局调度分发VCPU，全局调度需涵盖计算、网络和虚拟化层，从而在设计阶段给出跨主机与虚拟机的端到端时序保证。

Method: 设计一种基于VirtIO的虚拟网络，实现IEEE 802.1Qbv准兼容的虚拟机间通信；提出虚拟网络架构，并推导由方案特性产生的网络级调度约束；在ACRN hypervisor中实现该设计。

Result: 在负载网络中对关键流量进行端到端时延评估，表明该时间触发VirtIO-net原型在负载下仍能提供可预测的端到端时延。

Conclusion: 时间触发的VirtIO-net原型可在负载条件下为时间触发虚拟机间的关键消息提供可预测的端到端时延，从而支撑不依赖静态分区的端到端时序保证。

Abstract: Virtualization enables mixed-criticality applications to run on shared hardware by isolating them within separate virtual machines (VMs). However, achieving end-to-end timing guarantees for critical tasks hosted on virtual CPUs (VCPUs) without relying on static partitioning is hard. A possible solution is a time-triggered hypervisor that dispatches VMs' VCPUs to processor cores according to a predetermined global schedule. A global schedule includes the computation, the network, and the virtualization layer to provide end-to-end timing guarantees across hosts and VMs during design time. In this work, we propose a VirtIO-based virtual network that realizes IEEE 802.1Qbv quasi-compliant inter-VM communication, providing predictable timing for critical messages exchanged between time-triggered VMs. We present our VirtIO-based virtual network architecture and derive network-level scheduling constraints that emerge from the properties of the proposed solution. We implement the proposed design in the ACRN hypervisor and evaluate the end-to-end latency of critical traffic in a loaded network, demonstrating that our time-triggered VirtIO-net prototype provides predictable end-to-end latency under load.

</details>


### [23] [Shortcut in Generalization: Rethinking Evaluation Protocol for Encrypted Traffic Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.17961)
*Qiming Yu,Chungang Lin,Zhengyi Ma,Zijia Song,Yuliang Lu,Guozheng Yang,Dongzhen Qi*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 本文系统研究了加密流量分类评估协议中的捷径因素（字段级捷径、跨分区共享、训练-测试处理不一致），并通过特征重要性、重训练特征移除、字段级拆分敏感性、Source-IP重叠度与每流包数变化等实验，证明现有方法的性能受跨分区共享和训练-测试变换一致性的显著影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 加密流量分类通常只以分类性能作为主要评价指标，但报告的 performance 可能受评估协议中未受控的捷径（如捷径特征、跨分区共享）影响，而非反映可泛化的流量模式；此外，仅测试阶段的特征变换还会引入训练-测试不匹配。因此需要系统研究当前评估协议如何受这些捷径相关因素影响。

Method: 对比分析特征重要性、重训练后的特征移除、字段级拆分敏感性；变化 Source-IP 重叠度与每流包数；将仅在测试阶段的 IP 变换与训练测试匹配的变换进行对比。实验在三个公开数据集上进行。

Result: 现有方法的性能同时受跨分区共享和训练-测试变换一致性影响。将 Source-IP 重叠度从 0% 提升到 100%，在标识符掩码前准确率提高约 32–33 个百分点，掩码后仅约 8 个百分点。对两个 SOTA 预训练模型（YaTC 和 NetMamba），仅测试阶段 IP 置零带来的 11–13 个百分点损失，在训练与测试均施加同一变换后几乎完全恢复。

Conclusion: 当前加密流量分类评估协议中存在显著的捷径效应，报告的 performance 可能被高估；跨分区共享与训练-测试处理一致性是关键影响因素，评估时应加以控制，并采用一致的训练-测试变换以获得更可靠的泛化性能评价。

Abstract: Encrypted traffic classification is commonly evaluated under a range of experimental protocols, with classification performance reported as the primary measure. However, the reported performance may be influenced by shortcuts left uncontrolled in the evaluation protocol, such as shortcut features and cross-partition sharing, rather than reflect generalizable traffic patterns. And test-only feature transformations may further introduce train-test mismatch. In this paper, we study how current evaluation protocols are affected by shortcut-related factors, including field-level shortcuts, cross-partition sharing, and train-test processing. We compare feature importance, retrained feature removal, and field-only split sensitivity. We also vary Source-IP overlap and packets per flow, compare test-only IP transformations with matched train-test transformations. Experiments on three public datasets show that the performance of existing methods are affected by both cross-partition sharing and the consistency of train-test transformations. Specifically, increasing Source-IP overlap from 0% to 100% raises accuracy by about 32-33 points before identifier masking, compared with only about 8 points after masking. For two state-of-the-art pretrained models (i.e., YaTC and NetMamba), the 11-13 points loss caused by test-only IP zeroing is almost fully recovered when the same transformation is applied during both training and testing.

</details>


### [24] [CALM-5G: Event-Level Conformal Risk Control for Predictive Service-Assurance Alarms](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.17962)
*Ömer Faruk İşcanlı,Yağmur Sabucu Sandal*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: CALM-5G：一种在任意预测模型之上的服务保障层，基于合规性校准给出有限样本、无分布假设的告警保证，并在四个真实5G语料上验证漏报率与告警占空比的联合认证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 预测性服务保障的可部署性取决于“预测→告警”的转换环节，而现有方法缺乏有限样本、无分布假设的统计保证；同时需在保证漏报率合规的前提下降低告警占空比（即减少无效告警时间）。

Method: CALM-5G不依赖特定预测骨干，直接对服务级违规事件进行校准并对违规起始（onset）打分；采用样本外方式选择校准方案，每份认证漏报率同时报告告警占空比。评估使用四套独立语料：生产蜂窝网络、商用5G轨迹、开放式无线接入网试验床及自建5G独立组网试验床，并考察标签漂移下的批量与在线校准。

Result: 基于回归分数的风险控制在四套语料上得到0.24–0.91的占空比；交叉验证校准的起始定位在三套语料上使告警时间（time-in-alarm）下降56.3%–89.7%，第四套无显著变化；实际漏报率在三套上落在两倍标准误容差内，另一套落在一倍标准误内。标签漂移下，池化批量校准在生产语料上结构性超出容差10.9个标准误，而在线校准可恢复合规。拟合阈值的划分无法用于校准方案排序；当实体内部依赖使校准单元充足时，漏报率与占空比可联合认证。

Conclusion: CALM-5G将合规性校准与违规起始定位结合，能在多种真实5G环境下提供无分布假设的有限样本告警保证，并显著压缩告警占空比；但在标签漂移下需采用在线校准，且校准方案的可比性依赖足够独立的校准单元。

Abstract: Predictive service assurance converts forecasts of key performance indicators into alarms, and that conversion decides whether the assurance function is deployable. We present CALM-5G, an assurance layer that operates above any forecasting backbone and issues alarms with distribution-free, finite-sample guarantees. It calibrates on service-level violation events, scores violation onset directly, selects its calibration scheme out of sample, and reports the alarm duty cycle with every certified miss rate. Four corpora are evaluated separately, namely a production cellular network, commercial 5G traces, an open radio access network testbed and our 5G standalone testbed. Risk control on the regression-derived score yields duty cycles of 0.24 to 0.91. Onset targeting with cross-validated calibration reduces time-in-alarm by 56.3% to 89.7% on three corpora, with no significant change on the fourth. The realised miss rate stays within a two-standard-error tolerance on three corpora and within one standard error of it on the fourth. Under label shift, pooled batch calibration exceeds that tolerance structurally, by 10.9 standard errors on the production corpus. Online calibration restores compliance. Calibration schemes cannot be ranked on the split that fits their thresholds. Miss rate and duty cycle are certified jointly where within-entity dependence leaves sufficient calibration units.

</details>


### [25] [NetCoFi: A Budget-Aware Coarse-Fine Dual-Branch Model for Encrypted Traffic Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.17978)
*Zhengyi Ma,Qiming Yu,Chungang Lin,Wentao Cui,Zijia Song,Guozheng Yang*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 提出NetCoFi，一种预算感知的加密流量分类器，通过粗细两级计算分配实现精度与推理成本的灵活权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 加密流量分类对网络管理与安全至关重要，但实际部署需平衡分类精度与推理成本。现有模型基于固定流窗口处理，训练后计算量难以控制，在资源受限条件下限制了精度-效率权衡的灵活性。

Method: 利用字节表示与可观测数据包元数据，NetCoFi在细粒度上选择token邻域进行编码，同时将完整窗口压缩为互补的粗粒度上下文，从而在粗、细两个粒度上分配计算。采用多预算训练，使同一模型无需重训练即可在不同路由预算下运行。

Result: 在六个公开数据集上从头训练即达到最优分类性能；低预算设置下，相比同深度静态基线，在三个数据集上平均Macro-F1提升7.86个百分点；在DPDK-GPU流量处理流水线中，平均批吞吐量从26.42 Mb/s提升至43.42 Mb/s，较NetMamba提升1.64倍。

Conclusion: NetCoFi通过预算感知的粗细粒度计算分配与多预算训练，在保持先进精度的同时显著改善低预算精度与吞吐效率，为资源受限场景下的加密流量分类提供了可部署方案。

Abstract: Encrypted traffic classification is critical for network management and security, but practical deployment must balance classification accuracy and inference cost. Existing models typically process fixed flow windows with limited control over computation after training, which can limit the accuracy-efficiency trade-off under constrained resources. To address this problem, we present NetCoFi, a budget-aware classifier that allocates computation at coarse and fine granularities. Using byte representations and observable packet metadata, NetCoFi selects token neighborhoods for fine-grained encoding while compressing the full window into complementary coarse context. Multi-budget training enables the same model to operate at different routing budgets without retraining. Evaluation on six public datasets shows that NetCoFi achieves state-of-the-art classification performance when trained from scratch. Under low-budget settings, NetCoFi improves average Macro-F1 by 7.86 percentage points across three datasets, compared with a same-depth static baseline. In a DPDK-GPU traffic processing pipeline, it increases mean batch throughput from 26.42 to 43.42 Mb/s, a 1.64x improvement over NetMamba.

</details>


### [26] [Developments in Building Towards the Indian SKA Regional Centre](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.18091)
*Rohit Sharma,Yogesh Wadadekar*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 本文介绍SKA区域中心网络（SRCNet）向v0.2需求演进的架构，涵盖计算、编排与多层级存储，并阐述原型SRC基础设施的建设策略，重点描述印度节点（INSRC）的原型开发及算力与存储需求。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: SKAO数据产品需要全球范围的归档、分发与处理能力，SRCNet作为联邦式全球网络承担此任务；随着架构演进至v0.2，需明确新需求下的技术路径，并推动印度区域中心从原型走向正式运行。

Method: 以SRCNet架构向v0.2版本的过渡为分析对象，梳理计算、编排和多层级存储等关键需求；在v0.2框架下规划主节点与次节点的原型开发方案，并评估次节点的计算与存储资源需求。

Result: 提出了针对主、次节点的原型开发方案，量化了次节点的计算与存储需求，形成通向全面运行INSRC的路径规划。

Conclusion: 该工作为SRCNet v0.2架构落地提供了原型基础设施实施策略，并为INSRC的正式部署奠定了基础。

Abstract: The SKA Regional Centre Network (SRCNet) is a federated global network designed to archive, distribute, and process SKAO data products. This paper examines the evolving SRCNet architecture-focusing on the transition to version 0.2 (v0.2) requirements, including computation, orchestration, and multi-tiered storage-and details the strategy for establishing its proto-SRC infrastructure. We outline the prototype developments for the major and minor nodes in the context of v0.2. We describe the minor node compute and storage demands, establishing a pathway toward a fully operational Indian SKA Regional Centre (INSRC).

</details>


### [27] [Tanchun: Robust Flow-Based HTTP(S) DDoS Detection via Behavioral Attribute Guidance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.18406)
*Qingyang Li,Yunpeng Tan,Mingxin Yang,Yannan Hu,Lei Zhang,Yong Cui,Zongming Guo,Xinggong Zhang*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: Tanchun：一种仅依赖免解密流数据的漂移感知HTTP(S) DDoS检测系统，通过IP对聚合行为序列并借助可理解行为属性进行表示学习，实现鲁棒检测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: HTTP(S) DDoS攻击频率与规模激增，威胁网络安全。基于流的检测因免解密、可扩展而适合大规模检测，但流数据的粗粒度数值统计限制了检测精度，且在良性/攻击流量模式随时间漂移时鲁棒性不足。

Method: Tanchun按IP对分组流，并在连续时间槽上聚合为行为序列，捕捉时间访问模式以弥补单流信息不足；将序列抽象为人类可理解的行为属性以捕捉稳定的行为语义；随后对齐行为序列与其对应行为属性以指导表示学习，从而在流量漂移下实现鲁棒检测。

Result: 在多种HTTP(S) DDoS场景下评估，Tanchun取得最佳总体性能，平均F1-score为0.988；在良性流量漂移与攻击流量漂移下分别领先次优方法5.3%与9.6%；在真实流量轨迹上的实验进一步验证其实用有效性。

Conclusion: Tanchun通过行为序列与行为属性对齐的表示学习，仅用免解密流数据即可有效应对流量漂移，显著提升HTTP(S) DDoS检测的精度与鲁棒性，并具备实际部署价值。

Abstract: HTTP(S) DDoS attacks have surged in frequency and scale, posing a significant threat to network security. Flow-based detection is a promising solution for large-scale HTTP(S) DDoS detection due to its decryption-free operation and scalability. However, the coarse-grained numerical statistics in flow data limit accurate attack detection and lack robustness when benign and attack traffic patterns drift over time.
  In this paper, we present Tanchun, a drift-aware flow-based HTTP(S) DDoS detection system that relies solely on decryption-free flow data. Specifically, Tanchun groups flows by IP-pair and aggregates them over consecutive time slots into behavior sequences, capturing temporal access patterns to compensate for the limited information in individual flows. It further abstracts these sequences into human-understandable behavioral attributes that capture stable behavioral semantics. Tanchun then aligns behavior sequences with their corresponding behavioral attributes to guide representation learning, enabling robust detection under traffic drift.
  We evaluate Tanchun across diverse HTTP(S) DDoS scenarios. Tanchun achieves the best overall performance, with an average F1-score of 0.988, and outperforms the second-best method by 5.3\% and 9.6\% under benign-traffic and attack-traffic drift, respectively. Experiments on real-world traffic traces further demonstrate its practical effectiveness.

</details>


### [28] [An Optical KV Cache Network for Disaggregated LLM Serving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.18444)
*Seongwon Yoon,Shimeng Yu*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 该论文提出用光电路交换（OCS）网络传输分离式LLM服务中的KV缓存，通过在prefill计算期间预留并建立光路来隐藏机械OCS数十毫秒的重配置延迟。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 分离式LLM服务需在prefill GPU与decode GPU之间传输整段长上下文的KV缓存，在超额订阅的分组交换网络上会增加首token时延（TTFT）。OCS可为每次传输提供全速率电路且成本更低，但机械OCS需数十毫秒重配置，成为主要障碍。

Method: 提出预留调度器：在prefill仍在计算时就提交并建立电路，使电路在KV就绪时已就绪，从而将重配置延迟隐藏在prefill内。每个GPU通过自身的波分复用（WDM）光模块连接到OCS，仅占用一对光纤。

Result: 在真实服务trace与H100实测prefill时间驱动的GPU集群仿真中，该光KV缓存网络可匹配具备自适应路由的400G分组交换网络，成本仅为2:1超额订阅分组的0.55–0.78倍，并能容忍384端口机械OCS的50 ms重配置延迟。

Conclusion: 将OCS重配置延迟与prefill计算重叠，可在更低成本下实现与分组交换相当的KV缓存传输性能，使机械OCS在分离式LLM服务中可行。

Abstract: Disaggregated LLM serving moves each request's KV cache from a prefill GPU to a decode GPU before the response begins, and a coding-agent turn often moves the KV of its whole long context. Behind an oversubscribed packet fabric this transfer adds to time to first token (TTFT). An optical circuit switch (OCS) can give every transfer a full-rate circuit for less than an oversubscribed packet fabric costs, but a mechanical OCS needs tens of milliseconds to reconfigure. We hide this delay inside the prefill. A reservation scheduler commits each circuit while the prefill is still computing, so the circuit is ready when the KV is, and each GPU reaches the OCS over its own wavelength-multiplexed (WDM) optics on one fiber pair. In simulations of GPU clusters on real serving traces with measured prefill times on H100 GPUs, this optical KV cache network matches 400G packet fabrics with adaptive routing at 0.55 to 0.78 times the cost of a 2:1 fabric and tolerates the 50 ms reconfiguration delay of a 384-port mechanical OCS.

</details>


### [29] [Pedestrian Trajectory Prediction through LoRA Federated Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.18640)
*Simone Travagliati,Maria Luisa Merani*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 本文提出一种面向智能交通的行人轨迹预测分布式架构：联网车辆通过联邦学习微调基础模型，边缘服务器聚合参数，并采用低秩适配（LoRA）与增加本地训练轮次来降低通信开销，同时提出量化车-基础设施通信无线资源占用的新指标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在智能交通系统中，保护行人等弱势道路使用者是核心挑战。集中式训练难以获取分散在各车辆上的行人轨迹数据，且联邦学习方案面临车-基础设施（V2I）通信的无线资源开销问题，因此需要兼顾预测精度与通信成本的分布式训练方法。

Method: 1）构建分布式架构：连接蜂窝网络的车辆采集行人轨迹，基于联邦训练微调轨迹预测基础模型；2）蜂窝网络边缘服务器负责聚合车辆本地模型参数并更新全局模型；3）引入低秩适配（LoRA）降低无线资源占用；4）通过增加每轮通信中车辆本地训练轮次以减少更新次数与更新规模；5）提出新的品质因数（figure of merit）量化联邦方案所需的V2I通信无线资源使用。

Result: 所提方案在保持优异预测精度的同时，相比其他联邦训练方法显著降低了通信成本，验证了LoRA与降低车辆-边缘服务器通信周期性的有效性。

Conclusion: 微调、低秩适配与降低通信周期性三者的结合，是使车联网环境下分布式轨迹预测得以实现的关键；该架构可在保护行人目标下兼顾预测精度与通信效率。

Abstract: In Intelligent Transport Systems, protecting vulnerable road users, specifically pedestrians, is a paramount challenge. The present work contributes to this field, putting forth a distributed architecture in which vehicles connected to the cellular infrastructure collect pedestrians' trajectories and fine-tune a foundation model for pedestrian trajectory forecasting via federated training. A server positioned at the edge of the cellular network is responsible for aggregating the local model's parameters from the vehicles and updating the global model's parameters. To reduce radio resource utilization, Low-Rank Adaptation is proposed; to decrease both the number and the size of updates, an increase in the number of training rounds performed by the vehicles per communication round is put forth. A novel figure of merit is then introduced to quantify the use of radio resources dedicated to Vehicle-to-Infrastructure communications as required by the federated solution.
  The proposed scheme delivers excellent prediction accuracy while significantly reducing the communication cost compared to alternative federated training methods. This shows that combining fine-tuning, Low-Rank Adaptation, and a reduction of the communication periodicity between the vehicles and the edge server is key to enabling a distributed approach to trajectory prediction in vehicular environments.

</details>


### [30] [HILO: Enabling Low-power, Dual-Band Communication using Tunnel Diode Oscillators](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.18641)
*Dhairya Shah,Rajashekar Reddy Chinthalapani,Pramuka Sooriya Patabandige,Ambuj Varshney*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: HILO 是一种基于隧道二极管（Tunnel Diode）的双频段射频前端，通过利用而非抑制隧道二极管的谐波非线性，借助外部器件注入锁定基频并同时稳定高次谐波，从而用单一电路实现双频段收发，功耗低于 210 μW。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 无线通信是嵌入式系统中功耗最高的操作。低功耗发射机（如反向散射）虽可达到微瓦级功耗，但其信号弱，易受带内干扰和频率选择性衰落影响，尤其在非视距场景下。多频段传输可通过频域冗余（而非时域冗余）缓解上述问题，在不牺牲比特率或时延的前提下提升可靠性。然而，传统多频段收发机需要复制射频链路或高功耗频率合成器，难以适配超低功耗场景。

Method: 作者提出 HILO，一种隧道二极管前端，用单一电路实现双频段工作。其核心思想是反转传统范式：不再抑制隧道二极管非线性产生的谐波，而是加以利用。外部器件对振荡器进行注入锁定，使其锁定在基频，同时稳定一个高次谐波，从而在不需要第二个振荡器或倍频器的情况下获得两个注入锁定载波。发射模式下，HILO 利用自振荡混频同时调制两个载波；接收模式下，则在两个频率上采用自差（autodyne）下变频。

Result: HILO 利用隧道二极管的固有非线性实现了双频段链路，且整体功耗低于 210 μW。

Conclusion: 通过将固有非线性从缺陷转化为特性，HILO 以极低功耗（<210 μW）实现了双频段收发，为嵌入式系统中的可靠低功耗无线通信提供了一条新路径。

Abstract: Wireless communication remains the most power-consuming operation in embedded systems. Low-power transmitters such as backscatter achieve microwatt-scale operation but produce weak signals susceptible to in-band interference and frequency-selective fading, particularly in non-line-of-sight settings. Multi-band transmission can mitigate these effects through spectral-domain redundancy rather than temporal-domain redundancy, improving reliability without sacrificing bitrate or latency. However, conventional multi-band radio transceivers require duplicated RF chains or power-hungry synthesizers. We introduce HILO, a tunnel diode-based frontend that enables dual-band operation from a single circuit. HILO inverts the conventional paradigm: rather than suppressing the harmonics inherent to tunnel diode nonlinearity, it harnesses them. An external device injection-locks the oscillator to a fundamental frequency, simultaneously stabilizing a higher harmonic and yielding two injection-locked carriers without a second oscillator or frequency multiplier. In transmit mode, HILO exploits self-oscillating mixing to modulate both carriers simultaneously; in receive mode, it uses autodyne downconversion at both frequencies. By turning an inherent nonlinearity into a feature, HILO achieves dual-band links while consuming under 210 $μ$W.

</details>

*Sebastian Cochinescu,Ionut Moldovanu,Alexandru Constantin*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: Ghostline是一个基于6块ESP32-S3 Wi-Fi板与1台消费级路由器的无设备运动追踪系统，利用CSI构建最多36条有向Link/21个Span，通过自形成静默Baseline与投票生成匿名Track；三天黑客松期间在普通房间完成，单步行者试点显示中位轨迹误差0.85 m，但存在站立者丢失、空房间误报及无法区分两人的严重局限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索在低成本、现成商用硬件（ESP32-S3 Wi-Fi板与消费级路由器）上实现无设备（device-free）被动运动追踪的可行性，验证多节点网状CSI观测（节点间Link）相比传统星型拓扑能否提升追踪能力，并通过离线消融确认各处理阶段（Link集、网格投票、成像）对最终精度的实际贡献。

Result: 在线试点：标记间行走时Track距直线路径中位0.85 m，距基于提示的参考轨迹（提示间恒速、非实测位置）1.56 m（95% CI 1.29–2.83），46%的行走更新落在该参考2 m内；自由行走94%更新被追踪。局限：站立者在数秒内丢失；两个空房间时段误报Track比例达15.9%与2.9%；当日非正式测试无法区分两名步行者。离线消融：移除节点间Link且参数不变时，行走Tracked比例从90%降至19%，自由行走从96%降至60%，说明Track依赖Link集。相同per-Span证据、相同误报率下，普通RTI图像与网格+追踪器在2 m处相当（48% vs 47%），1 m处RTI领先（36% vs 21%），即成像阶段未带来可测精度增益。

Conclusion: 该试点证明：在黑客松级别的低成本现成硬件上可构建端到端无设备追踪流水线并获得粗略的匿名Track；节点间Link对追踪贡献关键，而独立的RTI成像阶段未提供可测精度提升。但结果受限于单步行者、无实测参考轨迹、站立目标丢失、空房间误报及无法多目标分辨，作者明确区分了试点所建立与未能建立的结论，未来需多目标与更严格真值评估。

Abstract: Ghostline is a device-free motion tracking system built on six ESP32-S3 Wi-Fi boards and one consumer router. The boards' Wi-Fi driver reports channel state information (CSI) for every received frame with the sender's address, and a modified open-source firmware forwards both, so every board carries a CSI stream for the router and for each other board: seven radios give up to 36 directed Links over 21 Spans instead of a star of six. Each Link is compared with a self-forming quiet Baseline, disturbed Links vote on a floor grid, and the grid becomes anonymous Tracks. The system was built and run during a three-day hackathon in an ordinary room. We describe it in full and report a pilot evaluation with one walker: spoken cues at seven marked positions, a two-minute free walk and five minutes of empty room, synchronised to video within 0.18 s (median). Walking between marks, the Track lay a median 0.85 m from the straight path and 1.56 m (95% CI 1.29 to 2.83) from a cue-based reference trajectory (constant speed between cues, not measured positions), with 46% of walk updates within 2 m of it; the free walk was tracked in 94% of updates. The limits: a standing person is lost within seconds, a false Track appeared in 15.9% and 2.9% of updates in two empty-room periods, and informal runs that afternoon did not separate two walkers. Two offline replays through the same pipeline show where this comes from: with the node-to-node Links removed and settings unchanged, the tracked share of walk updates falls from 90% to 19% and the free walk from 96% to 60%; a plain radio tomographic image on the same per-Span evidence, at the same false rate, matches the grid and tracker at 2 m (48% against 47%) and is ahead at 1 m (36% against 21%). On this recording the Tracks depend on the Link set, and the imaging stage adds no measurable accuracy. We state what the pilot establishes and what it does not.

</details>

TL;DR: 本文研究二维全息曲率可重构孔径 HoloCuRA，建立几何与阻抗耦合混合模型，分析曲率、互耦与方向相关可见性对方向性和频谱效率的影响，发现互耦主要影响局部方向性波动，而可见性主导全局方向图与频谱效率变化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 全息 MIMO 阵列具备高空间分辨率与灵活波前控制能力，但曲面或共形部署同时引入曲率、互耦（MC）与方向相关可见性问题，现有研究对这些因素的联合作用机理解释不足，因此需要建立统一模型加以量化分析。

Method: 针对二维全息曲率可重构孔径（HoloCuRA），采用基于几何的模型刻画阵元位置、导向矢量与空间相关性；同时构建基于阻抗的耦合矩阵与可见性向量，分别表征电磁相互作用与遮挡效应；在不同曲率下评估相对方向性与频谱效率，并考虑各向同性散射与 CDL-A 信道。

Result: 互耦主要改变局部方向性的波动幅度；可见性则在各向同性散射与 CDL-A 信道下均主导全局方向图与频谱效率的变化趋势。

Conclusion: 在曲面全息 MIMO 孔径设计中，可见性效应比互耦更具决定性影响，应作为曲率可重构阵列优化与性能评估的首要因素，互耦则可作为局部修正项处理。

Abstract: Holographic multiple-input multiple-output arrays provide high spatial resolution and flexible wavefront control, but curved or conformal deployments jointly introduce curvature, mutual coupling (MC), and direction-dependent visibility. This paper studies a two-dimensional (2D) holographic curvaturereconfigurable aperture (HoloCuRA). A geometry-based model characterizes element positions, steering, and spatial correlation, while an impedance-based coupling matrix and a visibility vector capture electromagnetic interactions and shadowing. Relative directivity and spectral efficiency are evaluated versus curvature. Results show that MC mainly reshapes local directivity fluctuations, whereas visibility dominates the global pattern and spectral-efficiency variation under both isotropic scattering and CDL-A channels.

Abstract: Moving extended-target sensing and tracking are studied in a beamformed PRS/OFDM ISAC framework. The target is modeled as a rigid cluster of scatterers whose Tx--target--Rx echoes coherently combine in a MIMO scattering channel. Delay, azimuth/elevation, and Doppler are estimated using PRS matched filtering with delay centroiding, single-cluster 2D-MUSIC, and FFT-based Doppler estimation, then used for independent localization and EKF-based tracking. The framework is evaluated in monostatic and bistatic modes under target-directed, receiver-directed, and omnidirectional beamforming. Simulation results show that EKF tracking reduces the aggregate 3D RMSE from 1.61 m to 1.38 m relative to independent localization. Target-directed beamforming substantially improves tracking accuracy, achieving 1.38 m RMSE compared with 5.91 m and 8.38 m for omnidirectional and receiver-directed beamforming, respectively. The results highlight the importance of target-aware beam management, delay-angle-Doppler fusion, and sensing geometry for mobile-target tracking in ISAC networks.

Abstract: Camera-based beam selection can reduce the need for exhaustive channel measurement in highly mobile millimeter-wave UAV links, but the complementary value of visual and position-derived representations and their behavior under modality degradation require further investigation. This paper develops a staged multimodal framework for current-beam prediction using real-world UAV measurements. The proposed 32-beam classifier combines an ImageNet-pretrained EfficientNet-B3 visual encoder with two deterministic position-derived representations: an explicit Geometric-Kinematic vector and a nonlinear synthetic feature basis constructed from position. The image and synthetic representations are fused first, followed by late fusion with the geometry representation. Experiments on DeepSense 6G Scenario 23 show that Image-Synthetic fusion achieves 86.92% Top-1, 99.03% Top-3, and 99.56% Top-5 accuracy, improving Top-1 performance by 2.64 percentage points over image-only inference and outperforming the 60.71% Top-1 accuracy of synthetic-only inference. Adding the Geometric-Kinematic branch yields the highest measured performance, reaching 87.14% Top-1, 99.12% Top-3, and 99.60% Top-5 accuracy. Controlled input interventions further demonstrate complementary behavior under visual degradation and strong sensitivity to severe synthetic-input corruption. Position-derived nonlinear features provide complementary information to visual context and improve current-beam prediction in the evaluated real-world UAV setting.
