TL;DR: 本文提出一种自适应波达方向（DOA）估计器，采用高斯核加权抑制脉冲噪声中的大幅值离群点，并基于KL散度准则在线调整核带宽，实现递归更新与DOA恢复。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统DOA估计方法依赖高斯分布或二阶统计假设，在脉冲噪声环境下易受大幅值离群点影响，性能显著退化，因此需要一种能自适应抑制离群点、且不依赖先验固定参数的鲁棒估计方法。

Method: 采用高斯核加权衰减大预测误差的影响；基于KL散度准则构造样本近似，导出递归的核带宽更新公式，使离群点抑制程度随观测误差统计自适应变化，而非预先设定固定带宽；空间滤波器系数与核带宽均递归更新，并从所得空间谱中恢复DOA；在标准自适应滤波近似下给出均值与均方分析，刻画系数收敛性与稳态行为。

Result: 在高斯混合脉冲噪声下的仿真表明：系数自适应稳定，DOA估计精度优于若干代表性对比方法；在低至中等信噪比区间增益最为显著；在线带宽自适应还降低了对初始核带宽的敏感性。

Conclusion: 所提方法通过核加权与KL散度驱动的带宽在线自适应，实现了脉冲噪声下鲁棒的DOA估计，兼具理论收敛性分析与实证性能优势，并减小了对初始带宽选择的依赖。

Abstract: Direction-of-arrival (DOA) estimation can degrade markedly in impulsive noise, where methods relying on Gaussian or second-order statistical assumptions are particularly susceptible to large-amplitude outliers. This paper develops an adaptive DOA estimator that employs Gaussian kernel weighting to attenuate the influence of large prediction errors while adjusting the kernel bandwidth online according to a Kullback--Leibler (KL) divergence criterion. A sample-based approximation of the KL criterion yields a recursive bandwidth update that adapts the degree of outlier suppression to the observed error statistics rather than prescribing a fixed bandwidth a priori. The spatial-filter coefficients and kernel bandwidth are updated recursively, and the DOAs are recovered from the resulting spatial spectrum. Mean and mean-square analyses are developed under standard adaptive-filtering approximations to characterize the coefficient convergence and steady-state behavior. Simulation results under Gaussian-mixture impulsive noise demonstrate stable coefficient adaptation and improved DOA estimation accuracy over representative competing methods in several challenging operating regimes, with the most pronounced gains occurring at low-to-moderate signal-to-noise ratios. The results also indicate reduced sensitivity to the initial kernel bandwidth through online bandwidth adaptation.

</details>


### [36] [UL-Assisted Parametric CSI Feedback and Prediction for FDD Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.14649)
*Hao Chang,Mingcheng Nie,Shuangyang Li,Chang Liu,Branka Vucetic,Yonghui Li,Wibowo Hardjawana*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 本文提出一种上行辅助的参数化CSI反馈与预测方案，用于时变参数的多用户FDD系统，通过对反馈符号与部分互易的上行参数联合解码来预测未来下行信道，并在Sionna光线追踪数据集上取得优于基线的预测性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: FDD系统中上下行信道不完全互易，UE需将估计的下行CSI反馈给基站；但信道在CSI获取与使用之间发生时变，导致反馈CSI在基站使用时已过时，影响下行传输性能。

Method: 利用上下行信道参数的部分互易性：基站先从UE发送的SRS中估计上行信道参数并广播下行导频；UE不显式估计下行信道，而是将观测直接编码为反馈符号回传；基站解码器将反馈符号与部分互易的上行参数结合，预测未来下行信道参数；最后由显式物理信道模型合成对应的下行信道矩阵。

Result: 在Sionna光线追踪（RT）数据集上的仿真表明，该方案在不同信噪比和不同预测长度下，信道预测性能均优于所考虑的基线方案。

Conclusion: 通过上行辅助的参数化反馈与预测，可有效缓解FDD系统中CSI反馈过时问题，提升时变信道下的下行信道预测精度。

Abstract: In frequency-division duplex (FDD) systems, the uplink (UL) and downlink (DL) channels are not fully reciprocal. Therefore, DL channel state information (CSI) estimated by each user equipment (UE) must be fed back to the base station (BS). However, because the channel varies between CSI acquisition and utilization, the feedback CSI may become outdated before it is used at the BS. To address this challenge, this paper proposes an UL-assisted parametric CSI feedback and prediction scheme for multi-user FDD systems with time-varying channel parameters. By exploiting the partial reciprocity between the UL and DL channel parameters, the BS first estimates the UL channel parameters from the sounding reference signals (SRSs) transmitted by the UEs. The BS then broadcasts DL pilots, allowing each UE to obtain observations containing information about its current DL channel. Instead of explicitly estimating the DL channel from these observations, each UE directly encodes them into feedback symbols and transmits the symbols to the BS. The BS decoder combines the feedback symbols with the partially reciprocal UL parameters to predict the future DL channel parameters. Finally, the corresponding DL channel matrix is synthesized from the predicted parameters using an explicit physical channel model. Simulation results on Sionna ray tracing (RT) dataset show that the proposed scheme outperforms the considered baselines in terms of channel prediction performance over different signal-to-noise ratios and prediction lengths.

</details>


### [37] [A QoE-Driven Framework for O-RAN Network Slicing Based on Empirical Multi-User VR Traffic Modelling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.14771)
*Ahmed Chyad,David Naseh,Maryam Hafeez,Syed Ali Raza Zaidi*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 本文提出一种面向Open RAN、由QoE驱动的网络切片框架，利用实测多用户VR流量统计来指导资源分配，实现PRB配额的动态闭环调整与准入边界分析。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VR对QoE要求严格，使无线资源管理更困难；静态切片分配浪费资源，而动态方法多依赖缺乏实证依据的启发式惩罚函数。

Method: 以经验获得的多用户VR流量统计指导资源分配：用闭式n重卷积建模n个并发用户的聚合数据速率与到达间隔（在最多4个用户上经验验证）；将拟合的数据速率CDF与实测吞吐量对比，得到基于百分位的满意度指标，并以到达间隔规律设定切片目标；在近实时RIC xApp中通过E2SM-KPM与E2SM-RC接口形成闭环，施加切片级PRB配额。

Result: 在空口测试平台上，从贪婪的相邻切片回收最多41%的小区承诺PRB，同时不减少受保护的VR切片；解析研究估计了不同用户数与信道质量下的PRB需求与单用户QoE，给出目标QoE无法达到的准入边界；对照实验表明固定速率合成负载将VR切片的PRB占用高估了68%。

Conclusion: 数据驱动的切片维度规划对VR QoE保障是必要的，实证流量统计可支撑更高效的动态切片资源分配与准入控制。

Abstract: Virtual Reality (VR) requires strict Quality-of-Experience (QoE) guarantees, making radio resource management harder. Static slice allocations are wasteful, while dynamic approaches often rely on heuristic penalty functions lacking an empirical basis. We introduce a QoE-driven network-slicing framework for Open RAN in which resource allocation is guided by empirically derived multi-user VR traffic statistics. An analytical study then estimates the PRB demand and per-user QoE across user counts and channel qualities, giving the admission boundary beyond which the target QoE is unattainable. A finite-battery model and an adaptive inter-frame energy-management policy are further introduced to balance decoding energy consumption and battery preservation. The resulting non-convex fractional problem is solved using Dinkelbach's method, alternating optimization, and semidefinite relaxation. Abstract: In integrated sensing and communication (ISAC) systems, strict sensing requirements can substantially constrain the transmit power available for communication. A hybrid reconfigurable intelligent surface (HRIS) with passive reflection and active amplification capabilities mounted on an unmanned aerial vehicle (UAV) can enhance coverage and strengthen communication links in such power-limited regimes. Meanwhile, rate-splitting multiple access (RSMA) provides an effective transmission strategy for managing inter-user interference, particularly in dense multiuser wireless scenarios. Motivated by this, we analyze the performance of a UAV-mounted HRIS-assisted monostatic ISAC system employing RSMA scheme. We consider the imperfections in the channel state information and successive interference cancellation in the RSMA, which are inevitable in practical system implementations. We then derive highly accurate expressions for outage probability, ergodic rate, and throughput to evaluate communication performance over Nakagami-$m$ fading channels. These expressions reveal key insights into the diversity order and coding gain of the system in the high signal-to-noise ratio regime. To characterize the sensing performance, we derive expressions for the probability of detection, the probability of false alarm, and the Cramér-Rao lower bound. Numerical results validate the theoretical findings and highlight the performance gains of the proposed system over conventional multiple-access schemes and RIS-based systems.

Abstract: In this paper, we consider joint beamforming and power allocation for a mobile multiuser MIMO integrated sensing and communication (ISAC) network with clutter, residual self-interference, and outdated channel state information, under per-user rate and sensing signal-to-interference-plus-noise ratio (SINR) constraints that must hold on every realization. The design must be recomputed every block, and distant or weak targets can make the sensing constraint unmeetable. We propose a physics-informed unsupervised learning (PI-UnSL) network that corrects the analytical design, meets the power and norm constraints exactly by construction, and is conditioned on closed-form interference statistics, spanning the whole tradeoff in one pass. We derive a closed-form sensing SINR at the interference-aware combiner, yielding a necessary range-radar-cross-section-power condition and a priority-independent analytic violation floor, so the problem is intermittently infeasible. We then show that a dual targeting below the attainable floor has no fixed point, and calibrate the label-free objective against the measured floor. Numerical results confirm the floor and the bounded calibrated dual, and show that PI-UnSL substantially raises per-realization joint quality of service over the closed-form baseline, delivers more rate than time-division ISAC at matched detection over most of the tradeoff, and exceeds the reliability of an uncalibrated alternating-optimization solver at a fraction of its latency. More broadly, the physical knowledge given to the network, not its architecture, makes such problems learnable without labels: five architectures fail alike from scratch and perform alike when started from the closed-form design and conditioned on its interference statistics.

  <summary>Details</summary>

Method: 1) 用瞬时链路平面精确刻画倾斜轨道与地球自转下的星地几何，给出斜距、仰角、时延与多普勒的闭式表达；2) 在该几何上推导仰角驱动的LOS/NLOS混合信道接收SNR密度，并引入分割相关的CSI老化机制，得到每个分割的未编码BER规律；3) 将分割选择建模为偏好集中化的可行性问题，约束为可靠性、老化与前传容量；4) 证明其最优解为四个状态坐标下的闭式区域规则，并与穷举枚举验证；5) 面向边缘xApp/rApp提出紧凑的KAN-LSTM预测器，提前数秒预测分割调度，并与朴素和反应式基线比较。

Result: 闭式区域规则与穷举枚举完全一致，无一例不匹配；基于KAN-LSTM的提前调度预测达到高调度准确率，BER违例概率低于1%，并能获得接近真实策略的goodput，性能优于朴素与反应式基线。

Conclusion: 分割之间的本质差异在于其自适应环路所获得CSI的新鲜度：地面闭环需承担馈线往返时延并产生由老化导致的误码地板。据此得到可解释的闭式分割选择区域规则，并用KAN-LSTM将反应式最优规则转化为可提前执行的预测式调度，在低BER违例下逼近最优goodput。

Abstract: In this paper, we study functional-split selection for an Open radio access network (RAN) deployed as a non-terrestrial network (NTN), in which a low-Earth-orbit (LEO) satellite hosts the radio unit, so that each candidate split (7.2x, 6, 2) places the RAN at a distinct point of the transparent-to-regenerative axis. We capture the space-ground geometry exactly through an instantaneous link plane, which yields closed-form slant range, elevation, delay, and Doppler for an inclined orbit and a rotating Earth. On this geometry we derive the received-SNR density of the elevation-driven LOS/NLOS mixture channel and, through a split-dependent channel state information (CSI) aging mechanism, an uncoded bit-error-rate (BER) law for each split, establishing that what separates the splits is the freshness of the CSI each brings to its adaptation loops: a loop closed on the ground pays the feeder round-trip and incurs an aging-induced error floor. We formulate split selection as a prefer-centralized feasibility problem over reliability, aging, and fronthaul-capacity constraints, and we show that its exact solution is a closed-form region rule in four state coordinates, validated against exhaustive enumeration without a single mismatch. Because this solution is reactive whereas a split change must be initiated ahead of time, we propose a compact Kolmogorov-Arnold network (KAN)-LSTM, sized for an edge xApp/rApp, that predicts the split schedule seconds ahead; we show that it attains high schedule accuracy at a sub-percent BER-violation probability and delivers nearly all of the ground-truth policy's goodput, outperforming naive and reactive baselines.

</details>

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 负荷分解中清单不完整会产生归因歧义：未认领需求的变化可以掩盖已认领负荷的变化，而重建误差对此不敏感。仅凭重建误差无法判断归因是否正确，因此需要研究允许的目标轨迹与干扰轨迹如何支配这种歧义。

Method: 用有界有序马尔可夫链建模未认领需求；用无权接受器（unweighted acceptor）从某一目标中剔除与调试不一致的路径，且不对幸存路径重新加权；用精确因子MAP联合解码所有路径。

Result: 在两栋全仪表化商业建筑中，仅限制外部照明支撑即可改善屋顶压缩机数量的吻合度，而并未约束任何压缩机链，且增益依赖于干扰表示方式。合成实验界定边界：在保持支撑的变化下效应持续，但当真实路径违反已调试支撑或已调试水平错误标定时效应反转。

Conclusion: 归因取决于目标—干扰路径的联合几何结构，而非仅取决于重建误差。因此不能仅凭重建误差评估负荷分解的归因正确性。

Abstract: An incomplete inventory creates a specific attribution ambiguity in load disaggregation: unclaimed demand can offset changes in claimed loads without altering meter reconstruction. We study how the allowed target and nuisance trajectories govern this ambiguity. A bounded ordered Markov chain represents unclaimed demand, while an unweighted acceptor removes commissioning-inconsistent paths from one target without reweighting surviving paths; exact factorial MAP jointly decodes all paths. At two fully instrumented commercial buildings, restricting exterior-lighting support improves agreement on the count of rooftop compressors although no compressor chain is constrained; the gain depends on the nuisance representation. A synthetic experiment identifies the boundary: the effect persists under support-preserving changes but reverses when the true path violates commissioned support or the commissioned level is miscalibrated. Attribution therefore depends on joint target-nuisance path geometry, not reconstruction error alone.

</details>

Main category: eess.SP

  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: URLLC对可靠短包传输与高效无线资源分配要求高，但用户数与天线数增加时，有限块长（FBL）感知波束赋形计算负担剧增；现有SCA方法依赖通用凸求解器，未挖掘波束赋形问题的结构。

Method: 以FBL和速率为目标、受发射功率与每用户最低速率约束；每次SCA迭代得到凸QCQP，并重构为等价一致性问题。对M天线基站服务N个IoT节点的场景，全局更新利用Kronecker结构，将MN×MN分解替换为共享M×M分解；利用秩一信道矩阵实现低复杂度局部投影。

Result: 与CVX-SCA在目标值、可行性与收敛性上高度一致；用户规模扩展时，端到端较CVX-SCA加速4倍以上，单次迭代较标准ADMM-SCA加速约6倍。

Abstract: Ultra-reliable and low-latency communication (URLLC) is essential for mission-critical cellular Internet of Things (IoT) applications requiring reliable short-packet transmission and efficient radio-resource allocation. However, finite-blocklength (FBL)-aware beamforming becomes computationally demanding as the numbers of users and antennas increase. Existing successive convex approximation (SCA) methods typically rely on general-purpose convex solvers without exploiting the structure of the resulting beamforming problem. This paper develops a structure-exploiting consensus alternating direction method of multipliers (ADMM) framework for low-complexity FBL-aware beamforming in downlink multi-user multiple-input single-output (MU-MISO) cellular IoT networks. The FBL sum rate is maximized subject to transmit-power and per-user minimum-rate constraints. Each SCA iteration yields a convex quadratically constrained quadratic program (QCQP), reformulated into an equivalent consensus problem. For an M-antenna base station serving N IoT nodes, the global update exploits Kronecker structure to replace an MN-by-MN factorization with a shared M-by-M factorization, while rank-one channel matrices enable low-complexity local projections. CVX-SCA serves as a general-purpose benchmark, whereas standard ADMM-SCA isolates the gains from structural exploitation. Numerical results demonstrate close agreement with CVX-SCA in objective value, feasibility, and convergence. Under user scaling, the proposed method achieves more than 4-fold end-to-end speedup over CVX-SCA and approximately 6-fold per-iteration speedup over standard ADMM-SCA, demonstrating its computational efficiency for cellular IoT resource allocation.

</details>

Abstract: Continuous-aperture arrays (CAPAs) provide a new electromagnetic transmission paradigm by directly controlling the source-current distribution over a continuous radiating aperture. This paper studies CAPA-enabled integrated sensing and communication (ISAC) with spatially extended sensing targets, for which the electromagnetic response over each target region is characterized by an aperture-to-region operator. Unlike point-target models, the resulting illumination operator may have infinite rank, precluding a priori finite-dimensional reduction of the transmit design. We formulate the joint communication and target-illumination beamforming problem in the continuous electromagnetic domain and develop an operator semidefinite relaxation (SDR). A key structural result is that the optimal transmit covariance operators remain finite rank even when the aggregate illumination operator has infinite rank. We further establish the tightness of the operator SDR and provide a constructive rank-one recovery of the information beamformers. Since the finite electromagnetic subspace supporting the optimum is generally unknown, we develop a dual-slack-driven adaptive mode-enrichment method that discovers the required modes from the full-space optimality conditions. The dual-slack violation simultaneously identifies missing modes and yields a computable certificate of the remaining full-space optimality gap. Global convergence of the enrichment sequence to the full-space optimum is established. Numerical results validate the finite-rank optimal structure and show that the proposed method approaches the full-space optimum with substantially fewer electromagnetic modes than truncated-SVD and spatial-Fourier representations.

Abstract: Semantic sensing extends integrated sensing and communication (ISAC) beyond physical parameter estimation by exploiting task-oriented representations from radio echoes. Nevertheless, existing multimodal sensing remains largely limited to vision-assisted radio physical parameter estimation or typically processes the visual and radio modalities separately, leaving radio representations underutilized for visual task enhancement. Feature-level fusion enables closer cross-modal interaction, while echo interference complicates the learning of user-specific radio representations and its semantic consistency with the visual entities. We propose a radio encoder to learn radio representations oriented toward visual localization from superimposed pilot echoes, together with Variational Radio-Visual Grounding (VRVG) to promote semantic consistency between the learned radio representations and visual spatial features. The radio representations are then formulated as semantic probes to highlight the corresponding entities in the visual scene through cross-modal fusion. Experiments using real-world RCooper dataset show that the resulting synergistic radio-visual embeddings enable accurate radio-visual user matching and significantly improve both user-specific visual localization and physical parameter estimation performance. The results substantiate the benefits of radio representation learning and semantically consistent fusion for multimodal semantic sensing.

Abstract: Multi-frequency electrical impedance tomography (EIT) often requires specialized hardware. This challenge is especially pronounced in biomedical applications. Leave-one-session-out matching located ABS inclusions in 29/30 runs at 160 kHz (87-97% across the band; chance 33%). For dialysis bags, polarity was correctly identified in all 36 nonzero-contrast runs. Signed voltage projections correlated with the cylindrical contrast factor (R^2=0.68 across runs; R^2=0.89 for the aggregate fit). Concentration was ordered (Spearman rho=0.85), despite saturation above ~10-fold contrast. Bag position accuracy ranged from 6/6 at the highest contrasts to 2/6 at matched conductivity. The matched-conductivity bag produced a ~1.7% RMS perturbation against 0.4-0.6% repeat-frame variability.

  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI-RAN 范式将 AI 与 RAN 负载共置于同一商用硬件上以增强下一代蜂窝网络的能力，GPU 是实现该范式的有前景平台。但通用并发 AI 应用会在 GPU 计算资源上与时延敏感的 RAN 相互干扰，因此需要一种既能保障 RAN 时延、又不牺牲 AI 性能的调度方案。

Method: 提出 Eunoia，一种动态实时 AI-RAN GPU 调度器；其设计对 AI 应用和 RAN 负载均透明，并在 RAN 时隙（slot）粒度上进行调度。作者将 Eunoia 部署于 NVIDIA Aerial AI-RAN 平台，并在空口（over-the-air）5G 专网与仿真多小区网络上进行评估。

Conclusion: Eunoia 证明了在共享 GPU 上以细粒度时隙级动态调度可有效解决 AI 与 RAN 的资源竞争：既满足 RAN 的严苛时延要求，又提升 AI 吞吐/性能，且对上层透明，适用于真实的 AI-RAN 部署。

Abstract: The AI-RAN paradigm makes the next-generation cellular network more versatile by co-locating AI and RAN workloads on the same commodity hardware. While the GPU is a promising platform for AI-RAN realization, a generic concurrent AI application interferes with the latency-sensitive RAN on GPU compute resources. In this paper, we propose Eunoia, a dynamic real-time AI-RAN GPU scheduler that is transparent to both AI applications and the RAN workload and operates at the granularity level of the RAN slot. We deploy Eunoia onto NVIDIA Aerial AI-RAN and evaluate Eunoia on both an over-the-air 5G private network and an emulated multi-cell network. We demonstrate that in a 27-cell AI-RAN, Eunoia keeps the RAN uplink P99 latency under 3 ms while delivering higher AI performance than other RAN-safe static partitioning methods.

</details>

Abstract: Due to the directional antenna pattern, the received signal power decreases when the target deviates from the array boresight, degrading direction-sensing accuracy. However, the different views lead to different direction-sensing accuracies. To fully exploit the complementary information from multiple views, two fusion methods, called SNR-weighted fusion and logistic-weighted fusion, are developed. The former directly assigns fusion weights according to the estimated received SNRs, whereas the latter employs an exponential SNR mapping to further emphasize high-SNR subarrays. Furthermore, the Cramer-Rao lower bound (CRLB) is derived to provide a theoretical benchmark for the proposed multi-view direction-sensing system. Simulation results show that logistic-weighted fusion achieves better accuracy in the very low-SNR regime, while both proposed methods approach the CRLB at medium and high SNRs and maintain stable sensing performance.

*Mingyang Yue,Xiaojun Yuan*

TL;DR: 该论文提出一种基于分数先验学习的概率框架，联合进行信道估计与干扰抑制，通过变分推断分离干扰信道与干扰符号，并结合分数涡轮消息传递和反向均值传播算法，配合子载波级协方差重建的IRC检测器，显著优于现有基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在MIMO-OFDM系统中，传统基于线性信道估计和子带平均干扰协方差重建的接收机在强干扰下性能严重退化。现有方法未充分利用干扰的双线性结构，且子带平均会带来性能损失，因此需要一种能有效抑制干扰并精确重建干扰子空间的新方法。

Method: 提出基于分数先验学习的联合信道估计与干扰抑制概率框架。构建变分推断问题，利用干扰的双线性结构，显式分离干扰信道与干扰符号。针对高度非凸的双线性推断问题，开发基于分数的变分推断算法：结合分数涡轮消息传递进行目标信道估计，采用反向均值传播框架进行干扰信道推断，干扰符号通过LMMSE估计更新。进一步设计子载波级协方差重建的IRC检测器，避免子带平均导致的性能损失。

Conclusion: 该算法通过概率框架与分数先验学习，成功解决了强干扰下MIMO-OFDM系统的联合信道估计与干扰抑制问题，子载波级协方差重建的IRC检测器进一步提升了性能，验证了方法的有效性与优越性。

Abstract: In multiple-input multiple-output orthogonal frequency-division multiplexing (MIMO-OFDM) systems, conventional receivers based on linear channel estimation and subband-averaged interference covariance reconstruction suffer serious performance degradation under strong interference. To address this issue, we propose a probabilistic framework for joint channel estimation and interference suppression using score-based prior learning. Specifically, we formulate a variational inference problem that exploits the bilinear structure of the interference by explicitly separating the interference channels from the interference symbols. To solve the resulting highly nonconvex bilinear inference problem, we develop a score-based variational inference algorithm that combines score-based turbo message passing for target-channel estimation with a reverse mean propagation framework for interference-channel inference, while the interference symbols are updated via linear minimum mean square error (LMMSE) estimation. We further design an interference rejection combining (IRC) detector with subcarrier-level covariance reconstruction based on the estimated interference channels, thereby avoiding the performance loss caused by conventional subband averaging. Numerical results demonstrate that the proposed algorithm is able to effectively suppress the interference in channel estimation, as well as to accurately reconstruct the interference subspace for interference rejection in data detection, and thus significantly outperforms existing baselines.

</details>

TL;DR: 本文研究数字信号处理（DSP）实现差异是否会在发射信号中留下可辨识特征，以五种生成2-FSK RTTY波形的软件DSP实现为例，用CNN对原始IQ、功率谱和瞬时频率进行指纹分类，最佳网络（IQ+功率谱）在实测短波记录上取得最高90%准确率。

  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统射频指纹识别主要利用发射机硬件缺陷（如I/Q不平衡、振荡器偏差等）来区分设备。本文动机是探究发射机的DSP实现差异（而非硬件缺陷）是否同样会体现在辐射信号中，从而为特定辐射源识别（SEI）提供互补信息。

Abstract: Radio frequency fingerprinting aims to identify transmitters by exploiting small differences in the emitted signal waveform. Common approaches exploit hardware-dependent imperfections for that purpose. This work instead investigates whether differences between digital signal processing implementations of a transmitter can be identified from their radiated signals. As a practical case study, five different software-based DSP implementations for generating a 2-FSK RTTY communication waveform are considered. In a monitoring receiver, a classifier can identify these DSP implementations using convolutional neural networks that are trained on augmented software-generated signals represented as raw IQ, power spectrum or instantaneous frequency. The trained networks are evaluated on over-the-air recordings obtained using a shortwave transceiver and a software-defined radio receiver. The best neural network combines IQ and the power spectrum representation and achieves an accuracy of up to 90%. The study further examines the influence of the signal SNR and the observation bandwidth on the classification accuracy. The results demonstrate that DSP implementations leave distinguishable characteristics in the transmitted signals and suggest that DSP-dependent fingerprinting can provide complementary information for specific emitter identification systems.

</details>

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: MA可提升ISAC的物理层安全（PLS）性能，但现有工作多假设通信用户与目标均处于单纯近场（NF）或远场（FF）模型。由于MA天线位置更新会引起传播场变化，实际场景中多个CU、窃听者与多个目标可能处于近远场混合环境，因此需要面向混合场环境建模并优化，以评估真实条件下的PLS性能。

Method: 构建混合近远场统一阵列响应模型（含连续NF/FF权重），在收发端联合设计发射波束成形与发射端天线位置，以最大化CU的和保密速率（SSR），形成高度非凸优化问题；提出两层算法迭代求解，结合投影梯度上升（PGA）方法与拉格朗日对偶变换。针对天线移动中NF与FF模型突变导致目标函数振荡的问题，将统一阵列响应与Barzilai-Borwein步长、Armijo线搜索及精确投影相结合，保证迭代中SSR不下降且目标函数在Rayleigh边界可微。

Result: 数值结果表明：采用混合场模型的MA-ISAC在SSR上与采用全NF模型几乎一致，且二者均优于FF模型；若在天线位置更新中错误使用FF模型，会造成MA增益损失，甚至可能使MA系统劣于使用正确模型的FA（固定天线）系统。

Conclusion: 混合场建模对准确评估MA辅助ISAC的PLS性能至关重要，所提两层算法可有效求解该非凸问题；忽略近远场差异（误用FF模型）会显著低估MA增益，实际部署中应采用匹配的混合场或全NF模型。

Abstract: Movable antenna (MA) can enhance the physical layer security (PLS) performance of integrated sensing and communications (ISAC). However, most existing works assume either near-field (NF) or far-field (FF) propagation models for both communication users (CUs) and targets. This paper considers a more practical scenario where multiple CUs, an eavesdropper (Eve), and multiple targets are in a mixed near- and far-field environment due to the changes in the propagation field caused by antenna position updates in MA with a unified array response and continuous NF/FF weights. To evaluate the PLS performance of MA-aided ISAC in mixed-field environments, we formulate an optimization problem to maximize the sum secrecy rate (SSR) of CUs by jointly designing the transmit beamforming and antenna positions at the transmitter. The problem is highly non-convex, so a two-layer algorithm is proposed to iteratively solve the problem using the projected gradient ascent (PGA) method and the Lagrangian dual transform. Since the abrupt switch between the NF and the FF models during antenna movement can cause the objective function to oscillate during iteration, the unified array response is combined with the Barzilai-Borwein step, Armijo line search, and precise projection to ensure that the SSR does not decrease during the iteration and the objective function is differentiable on the Rayleigh boundary. Numerical results show that the MA-ISAC using the mixed-field model achieves almost identical PLS performance to that of using the full NF model in terms of SSR, and both outperform the FF model. Using the mismatched FF model during antenna position updates results in a loss of MA gain, potentially making MA inferior to the FA-based system using the correct model.

Abstract: This paper investigates how bistatic delay information improves the observability and localization performance of 3D moving targets in distributed Wi-Fi sensing. Motivated by emerging IEEE 802.11bf WLAN sensing, we consider one transmitter, multiple distributed receivers, and a single target with unknown position and velocity. We compare Doppler-only sensing with delay-Doppler sensing, in which delay-capable receivers provide additional bistatic path-length information for target localization. We derive the Fisher information matrix (FIM) and the position equivalent FIM (EFIM) for both sensing formulations. The resulting observability analysis shows that Doppler-only localization generically requires six receivers, whereas delay-Doppler localization can become locally identifiable with three receivers under non-degenerate geometry. We further study mixed receiver architectures in which only a subset of receivers provides delay estimates. Simulations with Wi-Fi-like bandwidths confirm the transition and quantify the roles of bandwidth, Doppler noise, receiver geometry, and delay-capable receiver availability.

</details>

TL;DR: 该文驳斥"时间Dicke态定向辐射不对称等于破坏互易性"的解读，指出一阶相干等价于经典相控阵，真正量子特征出现在二阶关联，并提出量子天线证书及若干理论修正。

  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 文献常将量子点阵列时间Dicke态的方向性辐射不对称，直接解读为通过量子态制备即可破坏互易性。作者认为这一解读存在概念混淆：互易性关乎收发响应而非方向图对称性；同时缺乏对"该不对称是否可被经典手段复现"的辨析，因此需要厘清量子与经典贡献的边界。

Result: 方向不对称本身可由经典相控阵复现，故不构成互易性破坏或量子优势证据；单激发态在二阶关联上违反经典光子符合下界，构成真正的量子特征；给出紧凑的不对称度界、平面阵列正确镜像操作、修正后的集体衰减率与测向精度饱和律；全部结论经独立数值验证一致。

Abstract: A quantum-dot array prepared in a timed Dicke state exhibits directional radiation asymmetry, which has often been interpreted as evidence of reciprocity breaking through quantum-state preparation. We show that this interpretation is incorrect: reciprocity concerns transmit-receive response, not necessarily pattern symmetry. Moreover, the first-order coherence of any single-excitation state is exactly equivalent to the power pattern of a classical phased array driven by conjugate weights, making the observed asymmetry classically reproducible. The genuine quantum signature instead appears in second-order correlations, where a single-excitation state violates the classical lower bound on photon coincidences. We therefore propose a quantum-antenna certificate combining nonzero directional asymmetry with photon antibunching at the beam peak. We further derive a tight asymmetry bound, establish the correct mirror operation for planar arrays, correct the collective decay rate with dipole-dipole coupling, and derive a saturation law for direction-finding precision. All theoretical results are validated through independent numerical implementations.

</details>

Abstract: Open Radio Access Networks (O-RAN) empower operators with choice and customization through their disaggregated and interoperable architecture. However, ensuring interoperability in O-RAN presents challenges due to complex testing environments and evolving specifications. This paper addresses these challenges by proposing the Generative AI and Graph Neural Network-based Open RAN Testing and Automation (GNG-OTA) framework. GNG-OTA utilizes state-of-the-art AI techniques including generative AI, natural language processing, and graph neural networks to enable accurate, reliable, efficient, and automatic O-RAN interoperability and performance testing.

*Le Xu,Rajesh Shrestha,Xiao Fu*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 提出潜因子扩散框架用于空谱无线电地图张量补全：对单发射机空间损耗场与频谱特征分别训练两个独立扩散模型，嵌入张量因子，实现扩散后验采样恢复，降低训练复杂度、提升测量格式/感知模式灵活性，并给出可恢复性与样本复杂度保证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 无线电地图估计（RME）/频谱制图需从稀疏测量恢复多域信号强度，天然是张量补全问题。扩散模型虽为生成建模SOTA，但现有基于扩散的RME方法依赖大量训练数据、场景或测量格式变化需重训练、且缺乏可恢复性保证。

Method: 提出潜因子扩散框架：在单发射机的空间损耗场与频谱签名上分别训练两个独立扩散模型；将其嵌入无线电地图张量的因子中；基于扩散后验采样完成张量补全。

Result: 建立可恢复性与样本复杂度理论保证；数值实验验证方法有效性，并在训练未见城市环境中展现强泛化能力。

Conclusion: 潜因子扩散框架在降低训练复杂度、增强测量格式/感知模式灵活性方面有效，并具备理论保证与跨城市泛化能力。

Abstract: Radio map estimation (RME), also known as spectrum cartography, aims to recover radio signal strength across multiple domains (e.g., spatial and spectral domains) from sparse measurements, naturally leading to a tensor completion problem. Recent advances show that deep learning-based generative models can provide data-driven structural constraints for RME, yielding substantial performance improvements over traditional model-based approaches. Diffusion models (DMs) represent the current state of the art in generative modeling and hold strong promise for advancing RME. However, existing diffusion-based RME methods often require extensive training data, necessitate retraining when scenarios or measurement formats change, and lack recoverability guarantees. This paper proposes a latent factor diffusion framework for spatio-spectral radio map tensor completion. By training two independent DMs on single-emitter's spatial loss field and spectral signature and embedding them into the factors of the radio map tensor, the proposed approach enables diffusion posterior sampling-based recovery with reduced training complexity as well as enhanced measurement format/sensing pattern flexibility. Recoverability and sample complexity guarantees under the proposed approach are also established. Numerical experiments validate the effectiveness of the proposed method, showing strong generalization across city environments unseen in training data.

</details>

  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究面向短距微波耦合在脑组织介质中的传播特性，需要验证探头在仿脑体模和离体脑中的耦合响应是否一致，并评估测量系统的可重复性。

Result: 自由空间S11仿真与实测在5.78–6.86 GHz内一致，-10 dB带宽1.08 GHz，分数带宽约17.1%。约6.3 GHz处，有体模S21约-25 dB，空气中约-40 dB，差异约16 dB。离体山羊脑S21在5.78–6.86 GHz内与体模吻合，工作频带最大差异约1.5 dB，重复测量行为可重复。

Conclusion: |S21|的增大归因于完整的探头-介质-探头系统特性，包括加载、互耦、界面、几何、传播及材料损耗；该测量不能唯一确定组织本征机制。

Abstract: A printed tapered-slot coupling probe with a partially inserted 12-mm wire element is characterized for short-range microwave coupling through a brain-mimicking agar--gelatin--saline phantom and \textit{ex vivo} brain. Simulated and measured free-space $S_{11}$ responses agree over a measured $-10$-dB band spanning 5.78 to 6.86~GHz, corresponding to a 1.08-GHz bandwidth and approximately 17.1~percent fractional bandwidth. In a controlled two-probe experiment, the phantom was introduced and removed while the probe arrangement and measurement conditions were retained. This comparison was repeated under the same retained arrangement, with the same general $S_{21}$ differential behavior observed across repetitions. Near 6.3~GHz, measured $S_{21}$ was approximately $-25$~dB with the phantom present and approximately $-40$~dB in air, corresponding to an $\approx 16~\mathrm dB$. \textit{Ex vivo} goat brain measurements repeated more than five times showed reproducible behavior. Across 5.78--6.86~GHz, the \textit{ex vivo} goat-brain $S_{21}$ response closely followed that of the phantom, with an approximately figure-derived maximum operating-band difference of about 1.5~dB. The observed increase in $|S_{21}|$ is interpreted as a property of the complete probe--medium--probe system, including loading, mutual coupling, interfaces, geometry, propagation, and material loss. The measurement does not uniquely identify a tissue-intrinsic mechanism.

</details>


### [59] [A Novel Coexistence Scheme for OFDM and OTFS Waveforms for Uplink in 6G Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.16184)
*Badr Eddine Ouakouak,Salah Eddine Zegrar,Hüseyin Arslan*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 本文提出一种前向与后向兼容的OTFS与OFDM共存框架，通过在时延-多普勒域设计专用扩频矩阵，使两种波形在相同时间-频率资源上保持正交，从而降低交叉干扰。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 6G网络需支持OFDM难以承载的高级应用，OTFS因在高移动性信道中性能优越而成为候选波形；但在同一频段部署根本不同的波形无法利用二者间的相互关系，因此需要兼容共存方案。

Method: 提出前向兼容且后向兼容的共存框架，将多种OFDM波形选项与OTFS无缝集成于同一网络；在时延-多普勒域采用特殊设计的扩频矩阵处理OTFS数据，使OTFS与OFDM波形之间建立正交性，从而在同时间-频率资源共存时有效抑制交叉干扰。

Result: 与其他共存方案相比，OTFS波形的误码率性能最多提升6 dB；该方案具有更强的干扰鲁棒性，并接近可达速率上界；通过利用全部带宽和时延，提升了距离与速度分辨率，从而改善感知性能。结论由数学分析和针对上行场景的大量仿真支持。

Abstract: The upcoming sixth generation (6G) wireless networks are designed to support advanced applications that the current air interface based on orthogonal frequency division multiplexing (OFDM) cannot accommodate, necessitating innovations in waveform design. Orthogonal time frequency space (OTFS) has emerged as a promising alternative, offering enhanced performance particularly in high-mobility channel environments. However, deploying fundamentally different waveforms over the same frequencies fails to exploit the mutual relationships between them. In this paper, a forward-compatible yet backward-compatible coexistence framework is proposed, where various OFDM waveform options are incorporated while seamlessly integrating OTFS within the same network. The proposed coexistence paradigm involves utilizing a specially designed spreading matrix in the delay-Doppler (DD) domain to process OTFS data. The matrix is tailored to establish orthogonality between OTFS and OFDM waveforms, effectively reducing cross-interference when both coexist within the same time-frequency (TF) resources. Our results demonstrate up to a 6 dB improvement in bit error rate (BER) performance for the OTFS waveform compared to other coexistence schemes. The proposed approach also shows greater interference resilience and approaches the upper bounds of achievable rates. By utilizing the full bandwidth and time duration, it enhances range and velocity resolutions, leading to improved sensing performance. These findings are supported by mathematical analysis and extensive simulations for uplink scenarios.

</details>


### [60] [Grouped Sparse Bayesian Estimation for Near-Field Massive MIMO Channels](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.16195)
*Xingcheng Fang,Lan Tang,Wei Xv*

TL;DR: 本文从麦克斯韦方程出发推导近场大规模MIMO的电磁信道模型，发现散射簇在波数域形成块稀疏结构，并提出可见性感知的块稀疏贝叶斯信道估计算法，在有限导频下取得更低NMSE。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 近场大规模MIMO需在有限导频下精确估计信道，而传统方法多基于简化几何模型，且忽略了大规模阵列的空间非平稳性，导致估计精度不足。本文旨在建立更贴近物理的电磁信道模型并利用其稀疏结构提升估计性能。

The analysis shows that the singularity-associated components become more sensitive to environmental perturbations near singular states. The proposed model also reveals a limitation of conventional water filling (WF): power allocation based only on instan- taneous channel gains does not account for such instability. We therefore estimate singularity information from broadband MIMO channel state information (CSI) and develop a singularity- aware transmit covariance optimization method. Abstract: In unmanned aerial vehicle (UAV) networks, integrated communication and sensing (ISAC) is emerging as a key enabler to support extended and continuous communication and sensing capabilities for sixth-generation (6G) services among UAVs operating in swarms. In this paper, a comprehensive framework is presented to investigate the ISAC coverage probability of a reference UAV-base station (BS) in a UAV network. A fixed number of UAV user equipment (UAVUEs) and UAV-BSs are deployed in an aerial corridor modeled as a finite cylinder. The three-dimensional (3D) binomial point process (BPP) is used to model both the 3D spatial locations of the UAV-UEs and the UAV-BSs. By adopting realistic 3D third generation partnership project (3GPP) antenna patterns for both the UAV-UEs and UAV-BSs, the UAVs' boresight direction of the maximum antenna gain is modeled as a random unit vector. All UAV-BSs have sensing capabilities, so an arbitrary yet fixed reference UAV-BS acts as a monostatic radar to acquire sensing measurements associated with a target UAV-UE at a given time snapshot while the target UAVUE communicates with the reference UAV-BS. Consequently, considering both clutter and communication interference, exact analytical expressions for ISAC coverage probability are derived. Low complexity baseline analytical expressions are also derived by ignoring clutter interference and directional characteristics of the UAV-UEs. Finally, motivated by the vision for intelligent UAV networks, a DeepSets-based model is developed and trained to estimate the optimal location of the reference UAV-BS that maximizes ISAC performance given a spatial deployment of the UAVs. To the best of our knowledge, this is the first work to rigorously investigate ISAC performance in UAV networks while incorporating the directionality characteristics of the UAV antennas.

</details>


### [63] [A Reduced Greedy Minimum Distance Phase Shift Design for Quantized RIS-Assisted Spatial Modulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.16519)

TL;DR: 本文针对离散相移控制下的RIS辅助空间调制（SM）系统，提出一种直接以最小欧氏距离为目标的低复杂度RIS相移设计算法，通过整数参数控制贪心搜索规模，实现BER与计算开销的可调折中，性能优于相位对齐类基准。

  <summary>Details</summary>

Method: 提出面向RIS辅助SM的低复杂度相移设计算法，适用于实际离散相移控制。该方法在离散相移集合上进行缩减式贪心搜索，直接以最小欧氏距离为优化目标（而非最大化有效信道增益）。搜索开销由一小组整数参数控制，从而在BER性能与计算复杂度之间提供可调折中。

Abstract: Reconfigurable intelligent surfaces (RISs) have emerged as a key enabler for next-generation wireless networks, offering control over the propagation environment through nearly passive reflecting elements. When combined with spatial modulation (SM), an RIS shapes a finite set of noise-free signal points at the receiver, and the error rate performance under maximum-likelihood (ML) detection is governed by the smallest pairwise Euclidean distance among these points. In this paper, we propose a low-complexity RIS phase shift design algorithm for RIS-assisted SM under practical discrete phase shift control. The proposed method targets the minimum Euclidean distance directly through a reduced greedy search over the discrete set of RIS phase shifts, in contrast to existing approaches that align RIS phase shifts to maximize the effective channel gain and therefore do not directly address the dominant error events. The search effort is controlled by a small set of integer parameters, providing a tunable trade-off between bit error rate (BER) performance and computational cost. We evaluate the proposed method against continuous and quantized phase-alignment baselines, including a complexity-matched comparison against the most directly comparable benchmark. Numerical results show that the proposed method achieves a significant SNR gain over quantized phase alignment under the same discrete hardware model, and this advantage persists under imperfect channel state information.

Abstract: Ultrasound image quality depends on both the probe geometry and the transmit and receive processing, but engineers usually design the two separately. Physics-based simulators (Field II, k-Wave, MUST/SIMUS) compute the radio-frequency channel data but give no gradients, so design studies must use grid or black-box searches that become slow as the number of parameters increases. We present a fully differentiable end-to-end pipeline for plane-wave ultrasound imaging, with synthetic speckle phantoms, an analytical frequency-domain RF simulator, delay-and-sum beamforming and an extensible image-quality objective. Over 15 test phantoms, the optimized pitch increases the cyst gCNR from 0.55 to 0.66 (about 20%), and the speed-of-sound correction adds a further 0.05. The lateral point-spread function agrees with Field II within about 10%, and on 49 real CUBDL plane-wave acquisitions from five institutions, the framework finds the global speed of sound with a mean absolute difference of 16.6 m/s from the published corrections and phantom speed of sound.

Abstract: Wireless propagation modeling is foundational to the evaluation and design of modern communication systems. To achieve accurate wireless propagation prediction, recent learning-based approaches leverage scene geometry and materials, demonstrating promising generalization across transceiver locations and unseen scenes. However, these approaches remain largely confined to a fixed configuration of array size, receiver orientation, and transmit beam. In this work, we introduce RFWeave, a learned wireless propagation model designed to generalize across scenes, transceiver locations, and transceiver configurations. To accommodate varying array structures, RFWeave incorporates variable-length transceiver tokens that represent antenna elements individually. Furthermore, to capture the geometry between transmit and receive antenna elements, RFWeave aggregates their pairwise spatial relations into propagation tokens. A configuration-conditioned decoder predicts the spatial spectrum and received power. RFWeave is trained on a large-scale simulated dataset spanning 10,000 indoor scenes and more than 5 million channel records under diverse transceiver configurations. Extensive evaluations show that RFWeave achieves state-of-the-art performance across all evaluated generalization settings, improving spatial spectrum structural similarity (SSIM) by up to 15.7% and reducing received power mean absolute error (MAE) by up to 35.4% over the strongest baselines on unseen scenes. Additional experiments on measured DICHASUS data and in beam management further demonstrate RFWeave's broader applicability to real-world channels and downstream wireless tasks.

Abstract: Channel estimation for extremely large-scale uniform planar arrays (UPAs) requires exploiting near-field channel structure to reduce pilot overhead. This work develops a 2D block orthogonal matching pursuit (2D-BOMP) estimator that exploits this structure through rectangular coefficient grouping. We establish sufficient recovery conditions for noiseless and noisy measurements and compare the sufficient pilot-length requirements of 1D and 2D partitions. Numerical simulations demonstrate the estimation benefits of 2D-BOMP in estimating the near-field channels of large-scale UPAs, compared with the conventional 1D-BOMP as well as the polar-domain based OMP method.

Motivation: RIS技术可提升覆盖与速率，但其在卫星-VSAT下行链路（尤其是从VSAT侧增强贡献）中的实际实现仍存在关键工程空白。

Abstract: The introduction of Reconfigurable Intelligent Surfaces (RIS) technology marks a transformative era in modern communication systems, enhancing coverage and facilitating higher data transmission rates. While recent research has delved into the integration of RIS into satellite networks, a critical and practical gap persists concerning its implementation in the satellite-Very Small Aperture Terminal (VSAT) downlink, specifically amplifying contributions from the VSAT side. This paper addresses this gap by proposing a simple and engineering methodology to optimize RIS position and orientation, incorporating considerations for the antennas' radiation patterns and the system's topology to streamline optimization variables. The primary objective of this paper is to alleviate the double fading constraint in the reflect link without the need for active elements, achieved by leveraging the Fresnel zone of the RIS. The improvement power ratio depends on the frequency and the VSAT 's diameter. The results show that for a VSAT with diameter 1m the gain is up to 9dB at 2 GHz (S band) and up to 14dB at 4 GHz (C band). Additionally, the RIS channel equation is refined, encompassing the tropospheric attenuation and accounting for electromagnetic wave polarization.

</details>


### [69] [Generative Deep Learning for CSI Feedback in Massive MIMO via Channel Distribution Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.16796)
*Liujia Yao,Changsheng You,Chao Zhou,Yi Gong,Chengwen Xing*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 本文提出预测辅助编码（PAC）框架，用于生成式CSI反馈：以预测的条件分布对下一帧CSI token做变长熵编码，兼顾压缩率与对UE运动突变的鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于深度学习的CSI反馈方法通过将高维CSI压缩为低维表示，或利用历史CSI预测未来CSI来降低反馈开销。但当用户设备（UE）运动状态突然改变时，这类方法会变得不可靠，性能显著下降。

Method: 提出PAC（prediction-aided coding）框架，自适应地基于预测的通道/CSI分布与实时观测CSI进行编码。每个CSI帧被表示为多个不依赖历史CSI获得的CSI token，使token生成对UE运动突变具有鲁棒性。第一阶段：根据历史token预测下一帧CSI token的条件分布（而非确定值）；第二阶段：下一帧到达后，利用该预测分布对其CSI token进行变长熵编码。为降低开销与推理时延，两阶段均做了高效设计：第一阶段采用定制的因果Transformer与新的位置嵌入策略来精确预测下一帧token概率；第二阶段采用并行因果解码策略，降低顺序token解码带来的推理时延。

Result: 大量实验表明，与现有历史感知基线相比，PAC将反馈开销降低约72%~84%，同时获得更高的重建精度；此外，在随机游走轨迹下PAC仍能保持CSI重建精度，而现有历史感知基线则出现明显精度退化。

Conclusion: PAC通过"预测条件分布+变长熵编码"的生成式反馈范式，在显著降低反馈开销的同时提升重建精度，并解决了UE运动突变导致历史感知方法失效的鲁棒性问题。

Abstract: Existing deep-learning-based channel state information (CSI) feedback methods reduce feedback overhead by compressing high-dimensional CSI into low-dimensional representations and/or exploiting historical CSI to predict future CSI. However, these methods may become unreliable when user equipment (UE) suddenly changes its motion. To address this issue, we propose a prediction-aided coding (PAC) framework for generative CSI feedback, which adaptively encodes CSI based on predicted channel/CSI distributions and the observed CSI in real time. Each CSI frame is represented by multiple CSI tokens obtained without using historical CSI, making token generation robust to abrupt UE motion. In the first stage, the conditional distribution, instead of a deterministic value, of the next-frame CSI tokens is predicted based on historical tokens. In the second stage, once the next frame arrives, the predicted distribution is used for variable-length entropy coding of its CSI tokens. To further reduce feedback overhead and inference latency, we propose efficient designs for both stages. For the first stage, a customized causal Transformer with a new positional embedding strategy accurately predicts next-frame token probabilities. For the second stage, a parallel causal decoding strategy reduces the inference latency caused by sequential token decoding. Extensive experiments show that, compared with existing history-aware baselines, PAC reduces feedback overhead by approximately 72% to 84% while achieving higher reconstruction accuracy. Additionally, PAC maintains CSI reconstruction accuracy under random-walk trajectories, whereas existing history-aware baselines suffer sharp degradation in reconstruction accuracy.

</details>


### [70] [Practical Hardware-Efficient ISAC: Enabling Multi-Target Sensing With Few RF Chains](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.16810)
*Zhenkun Zhang,Cunhua Pan,Hong Ren,Jun Tang,Dongming Wang,Jiangzhou Wang*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 针对混合模数阵列ISAC中RF链路少导致的多目标估计难题，提出突发波束扫描MIMO-OFDM框架，复用下行参考信号实现距离、径向速度、俯仰角与方位角联合估计，并推导联合CRLB与3D定位界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 混合模数阵列可降低ISAC硬件成本，但空间压缩与波束相关扫描响应使得在少量RF链路条件下进行多目标估计变得困难。

Method: 提出突发波束扫描MIMO-OFDM框架，复用下行参考信号；采用结构化全局-局部双张量估计器，先以扫描观测分离目标，再从选定波束的RF链路响应中精化参数，并以时延-多普勒特征维持跨阶段目标对应；由恢复的子阵列响应通过精确和差关系给出闭式俯仰角与方位角估计，避免小角近似和精细角度网格搜索；进一步用空间掩膜辅助加权最小二乘利用更长帧间时间孔径提升多普勒精度，并用帧内估计解决相位模糊；推导联合多目标CRLB及相应3D定位界。

Result: 仿真表明，所提方案在高发射功率下达到接近CRLB的估计精度，仅用四条RF链路即可实现厘米至毫米级3D定位，同时保持较低计算成本。

Conclusion: 该框架在少RF链路的混合模数阵列ISAC中实现高精度多目标联合参数估计与3D定位，兼具近CRLB性能与低计算开销。

Abstract: Hybrid analog--digital arrays reduce hardware cost in integrated sensing and communications (ISAC), but spatial compression and beam-dependent scan responses complicate multi-target estimation with few radio-frequency (RF) chains. To address these challenges, this paper proposes a burst beam-scanning multiple-input multiple-output orthogonal frequency-division multiplexing (MIMO-OFDM) framework that reuses downlink reference signals for joint range, radial-velocity, elevation, and azimuth estimation. Specifically, a structured global--local dual-tensor estimator separates targets by using scanning observations and refines their parameters from selected-beam RF-chain responses, with delay--Doppler signatures maintaining cross-stage target correspondence. The recovered subarray responses yield closed-form elevation and azimuth estimates through exact sum--difference relations, avoiding small-angle approximations and fine angular grid searches. To further improve Doppler accuracy, spatial-mask-aided weighted least squares exploits the longer inter-burst temporal aperture while resolving phase ambiguity using intra-burst estimates. The joint multi-target Cramér--Rao lower bound (CRLB) and corresponding three-dimensional (3D) positioning bound are also derived to characterize estimation performance. Simulation results show that the proposed scheme achieves near-CRLB estimation accuracy at high transmit powers and centimeter- to millimeter-level 3D positioning with only four RF chains, while maintaining low computational cost.

</details>


### [71] [Scattering-Mediated NLOS Ultraviolet Communication: Channel Characterization, Modeling, and Data Transmission](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.16964)

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 太阳盲紫外光无线通信可借助大气散射实现非视距传输，但实际扩散光束实验中残余LOS分量会影响信道特性，需要区分LOS遮挡与开放场条件下的真实散射信道表现。

Method: 开展100米户外UVC链路的对比实验（LOS遮挡与开放场），并构建考虑真实收发端特性与障碍物几何的光子追踪蒙特卡洛仿真框架以复现信道特征，采用OOK与PPM调制进行传输验证。

Result: LOS遮挡散射链路路径损耗显著更高、带宽更窄；MC仿真能高精度复现实测信道；在100米LOS遮挡链路上实现20-200 kbps可靠传输。

Conclusion: 该工作给出了迄今报道的最长距离、最高数据率的显式LOS遮挡散射介导UVC链路实验演示，验证了散射信道在NLOS场景下的通信可行性。

Abstract: Solar-blind ultraviolet (UV) optical wireless communication (OWC) enables non-line-of-sight (NLOS) transmission through atmospheric scattering. This work presents a comprehensive experimental and simulation study of a 100 m outdoor UVC link with comparative measurements under LOS-blocked and open-field conditions. The results show that LOS-blocked scattering-mediated links exhibit substantially higher path loss and narrower bandwidth than corresponding open-field measurements, highlighting the impact of residual LOS components in practical diffused beam UVC experiments. A photon-tracing Monte Carlo (MC) framework incorporating realistic transmitter/receiver characteristics and obstacle geometry is further developed to reproduce the measured channel characteristics with high accuracy. Despite the severe path loss and pulse broadening effects, reliable 20-200 kbps transmission is experimentally demonstrated over the 100 m LOS-blocked link using on-off keying (OOK) and pulse-position modulation (PPM), representing the longest-distance and highest-data-rate experimental demonstration of an explicitly LOS-blocked scattering-mediated UVC link reported to date.

</details>

Method: 构建太阳盲UVC四类基本信道的分类体系（taxonomy），并辅以代表性实验验证，开展信道间的对比性能分析。

Conclusion: 所提框架可用于指导未来太阳盲UVC系统的信道架构选择，提供实用性的设计准则。

Abstract: Solar-blind ultraviolet communication (UVC) exploits near-zero solar background radiation and strong atmospheric scattering in the 200-280 nm band, enabling photon-level daytime communication and propagation modes beyond conventional line-of-sight (LOS) links. However, existing studies mainly focus on individual channel types, and a unified channel classification is still lacking. This paper presents a taxonomy of all four fundamental solar-blind UVC channels together with representative experimental validations and a comparative performance analysis. The proposed framework provides practical guidelines for selecting appropriate channel architectures for future solar-blind UVC systems.

</details>


### [73] [On the CSIT-Limited Delay Robustness of IR-HARQ in Finite Blocklength RSMA Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.17199)
*Shaima Abidrabbu*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 该文研究有限块长下、非理想CSIT时功率受限RSMA配合可变长IR-HARQ的吞吐量与可靠性，揭示非理想CSIT导致的严格有限时延容忍上限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在功率受限、有限块长传输场景中，RSMA与IR-HARQ结合可提升可靠性，但实际系统存在非理想CSIT。作者希望探究非理想CSIT对该方案时延容忍与可靠性的根本限制。

Method: 推导公共流与私有流译码失败概率的解析表达式及紧近似，通过统一的CSIT退化因子同时刻画有限块长与CSIT不确定性，并用仿真验证分析。

Result: 完美CSIT下时延容忍随SNR趋于自然上限；非理想CSIT则给出仅由CSIT质量决定、发射功率无法突破的严格更低有限上限。残余干扰在高SNR饱和有效流功率，限制可靠性并造成该时延容忍上限。

Conclusion: 非理想CSIT为功率受限RSMA-IR-HARQ设定不可逾越的时延容忍上限，仿真验证分析并为非理想CSIT下时延鲁棒RSMA设计提供指导。

Abstract: This letter studies the throughput and reliability of power-constrained rate-splitting multiple access (RSMA) with variable-length incremental-redundancy hybrid automatic repeat request (IR-HARQ) under finite blocklength (FBL) transmission and imperfect channel state information at the transmitter (CSIT). While perfect-CSIT feedback-delay tolerance approaches a natural ceiling as signal-to-noise ratio (SNR) grows, we show imperfect CSIT imposes a strictly lower, finite ceiling set solely by CSIT quality, one that transmit power cannot overcome. We derive analytical expressions and tight approximations for common- and private-stream decoding failure probabilities, capturing FBL and CSIT uncertainty via a unified CSIT-degradation factor. Also, we show that residual interference saturates effective stream power at high SNR, limiting reliability and producing this delay-tolerance ceiling. Simulations validate the analysis and quantify the ceiling, offering design insights for delay-robust RSMA under imperfect CSIT.

</details>


### [74] [Colored Graph Signal Diffusion Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.17282)
*Yiğit Berkay Uslu,Samar Hadou,Shirin Saeedi Bidokhti,Alejandro Ribeiro*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 该论文提出一类"着色"分数扩散模型，通过正谱速率耦合漂移与扩散，为图频带提供可调时钟，统一了标准与图感知扩散，并用GSO值EDM预条件将去噪器分解为Wiener头与GNN。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准各向同性噪声扩散并非谱中性：由于各频带以不同速率丢失信噪比，模型从非均匀数据谱中继承了粗到细的生成层级。在图信号上，相关谱由图移位算子（GSO）决定。现有扩散设计默认接受了这一层级，却缺乏显式调控手段，因此需要一种能将该层级暴露为可调轴的方法。

Method: 构造一族着色分数扩散：将终端先验固定为由GSO着色的高斯分布，经由一个正谱速率耦合漂移与扩散，该速率充当每个图频带的"时钟"，决定各频带丢失与恢复信息的时机。标准扩散与若干图感知扩散的前向过程均为该族成员。进一步采用GSO值EDM预条件，将去噪器拆分为解析Wiener头（承载高斯二阶结构）与GNN（学习剩余非高斯依赖）。

Result: 数值实验表明，着色扩散在远少于标准扩散的采样步数下即可达到相同的生成质量。

Conclusion: 通过谱速率这一可调时钟，该工作统一了标准与图感知扩散的前向过程，并给出了去噪器的解析—学习混合分解，显著提升了采样效率，为图上的生成建模提供了可控的谱层级视角。

Abstract: Standard diffusion processes with isotropic noise corruption are not spectrally neutral. They inherit a coarse-to-fine generation hierarchy from the nonuniform data spectrum, because each frequency band loses signal-to-noise ratio (SNR) at its own rate. For signals on a graph, the relevant spectrum is that of the graph shift operator (GSO). A natural construction, therefore, builds both the diffusion dynamics and the denoiser from graph filters of the GSO, and any such design also takes a position on this hierarchy. We expose this hierarchy as a tunable axis through a family of colored score-based diffusions. Fixing the terminal prior to a Gaussian colored by the GSO couples the drift and diffusion through a positive spectral rate, which acts as a clock for each graph-frequency band. The clock sets when a band loses and recovers information, and the forward processes of standard and certain graph-aware diffusions appear as members of this family. A GSO-valued EDM preconditioning splits the denoiser into an analytic Wiener head that carries the Gaussian second-order structure and a graph neural network (GNN) that learns the remaining non-Gaussian dependencies. Numerical experiments show that colored diffusions reach a given generation quality in far fewer sampling steps than standard diffusion.

</details>


### [75] [On the Geometry of Radar Waveform Design with Constraints](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.17290)
*Hazel Brenner,Katelyn Hampel,Matthew Pollard*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 本文将信号处理中的非凸约束优化问题重构为黎曼流形上的无约束优化，介绍所需的光滑优化理论并适配雷达信号设计场景，在两个问题上与凸松弛方法对比验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 信号处理中的许多优化问题基于数值序列构成的向量/矩阵，常因非线性约束而本质非凸。传统做法是采用凸松弛以获得计算简单的算法，但会在解空间上带来可观的性能损失。若问题的约束能由黎曼流形结构隐式满足，则可直接在流形上以无约束方式进行优化，从而获得比凸松弛更大的灵活性和更高的效率。

Method: 引入黎曼光滑优化所需的最小理论框架，并将符号体系适配到雷达信号设计语境；当约束可由流形结构隐式满足时，将欧氏空间中的约束优化问题重铸为黎曼流形上的无约束优化问题。

Result: 将该方法应用于两个不同问题，并与同期基于凸松弛的信号设计文献进行直接并行对比。

Conclusion: 在可行条件下，基于黎曼流形的无约束优化可替代凸松弛途径，在雷达信号设计等场景中提供更强的灵活性与更高的效率。

Abstract: Many optimization problems that arise in signal processing applications are based around optimizing vectors/matrices of numeric sequences. These problems are often inherently non-convex due to non-linear constraints. Such problems are frequently solved through convex relaxations of the original problem to allow for computationally simple algorithms to be leveraged, albeit with the potential for appreciable performance costs in the solution space. When the constraints of a problem can be implicitly satisfied through the structure of a Riemannian manifold, optimizing directly on said manifold affords the opportunity to solve the problem in an unconstrained fashion. Therefore, when feasible, recasting the constrained optimization problem in Euclidean space as an unconstrained optimization over a Riemannian manifold offers greater flexibility and improved efficiency over convex relaxation based approaches. This paper introduces the minimal smooth optimization theory required and adapts the notation to a radar signal design context. In addition, we apply our approach to two different problems in direct parallel to contemporary convex relaxation-based signal design literature.

</details>


### [76] [Optimal Gating for Dead-Time-Constrained Single-Photon Detectors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.17421)
*Frederic J. N. Jorgensen,Steven G. Johnson*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 该文提出基于Fisher信息的框架，用于优化周期实验中的单光子探测器门控，取代以往经验性或启发式门控选择，并在激光雷达仿真中显著降低测距方差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 单光子探测器在每次探测后存在死时间，早期背景探测可能遮蔽后续信号；门控通过选择性停用探测器来保留对信息光子（informative photons）的可用性。既有门控选择多依赖经验或启发式，缺乏面向估计性能的优化框架。

Method: 建立Fisher信息框架以优化周期实验中的单光子探测器门控；分析探测器可激活频率的可行域，刻画为凸多面体（convex polytope）；构造自适应门控规则，在无物理参数先验知识下渐近达到最优“oracle”设计；推导实用准则以量化门控和/或光子衰减相对于无衰减自由运行采集的潜在增益。

Result: 在高堆积（high-pile-up）距离衰减激光雷达仿真中，所提出的数据无关（data-independent）门控设计将最坏情况渐近测距方差降低超过一半，优于自由运行采集；可行激活频率可由凸多面体刻画；自适应规则可在无先验参数下渐近获得最优oracle设计的性能。

Conclusion: 该工作将门控设计从经验选择提升为基于Fisher信息的优化问题，给出可行域刻画、自适应最优规则及量化增益准则，并在高堆积激光雷达场景中验证门控相对自由运行采集具有显著方差优势。（注意：上述摘要未提供完整论文，无法进一步核验推导细节与实验设置。）

Abstract: We develop a Fisher-information framework for optimizing single-photon detector gating in periodic experiments, in contrast to previous more empirical or heuristic gating choices. After each photon detection, such a detector is insensitive for a dead time, so that an early background detection can obscure a later signal. Gating preserves availability for informative photons by selectively deactivating the detector. In our high-pile-up simulation of distance-attenuated lidar, our practical data-independent gating design reduces the worst-case asymptotic range variance by more than half relative to the free-running acquisition (which does not use any gating). More generally, we characterize achievable detector-active frequencies as a convex polytope and construct an adaptive rule that asymptotically attains an optimal "oracle" design without prior knowledge of the physical parameters. We also derive practical criteria that quantify the potential gains of gating and/or photon attenuation over unattenuated free-running acquisition.

</details>


### [77] [Energy-Efficient RF Chain Activation for Uplink mmWave Cell-Free Massive MIMO Systems: A Minimum-Cost Maximum-Flow Approach](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.17478)
*Bo Yu,Abdol Hamid Aghvami,Mohammad Shikh-Bahaei*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于最小费用最大流（MCMF）的自适应射频链路激活（ARFA）方案，用于提升上行毫米波无蜂窝大规模MIMO系统的总能效。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 面向绿色可持续通信需求，能效成为6G网络设计的核心指标。无蜂窝毫米波大规模MIMO虽可通过大规模天线阵列获得吞吐量增益，但射频链路功耗高。自适应射频链路激活可在不严重损失速率的前提下降低功耗，因此需要设计有效的ARFA方案以最大化总能效。

Method: 将上行毫米波无蜂窝大规模MIMO系统的射频链路激活问题建模为最小费用最大流（MCMF）问题，并据此提出MCMF-based ARFA方案，以减少不必要功耗并最大化总能效。

Result: 仿真结果表明，所提方案在保持相对较高可达速率和可接受计算复杂度的同时，能显著提升系统总能效。

Conclusion: 基于MCMF的ARFA方案是提升6G无蜂窝毫米波大规模MIMO系统能效的有效手段，在能效、速率与复杂度之间取得了良好折中。

Abstract: With the incessant demand for green and sustainable communications, energy efficiency (EE) has become a cornerstone of sixth-generation (6G) network design, especially for cell-free millimeter-wave (mmWave) massive multiple-input multiple-output (MIMO) systems, which can achieve significant throughput gains by leveraging large-scale antenna arrays. Adaptive radio frequency (RF) chain activation (ARFA) is a key technique for reducing power consumption without severely degrading the achievable rate, thereby enhancing the total EE of 6G MIMO systems. In this paper, we propose a minimum-cost maximum-flow (MCMF)-based ARFA scheme to reduce unnecessary power consumption and maximize the total EE of uplink mmWave cell-free massive MIMO systems. Simulation results indicate that the proposed approach can significantly improve the total EE while maintaining a relatively high achievable rate and acceptable computational complexity.

</details>


### [78] [Timing Misalignment and Its Impact on OTFS and Other Delay-Based Waveforms](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.17554)
*Badr Eddine Ouakouak,Salah Eddine Zegrar,Hüseyin Arslan*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 该文统一分析了基于时延域的波形（OTFS、ODDM、OTSM、OCDM）在多用户上行链路中的定时失配（TM）问题，揭示了干扰抑制与保护开销之间的基本权衡，并与OFDM在多普勒扩展下的表现进行了类比和基准对比。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 面向6G的超可靠与大规模机器类通信需要新型波形。OTFS、ODDM、OTSM、OCDM等候选波形均利用时延域传输数据，在高移动性与感知场景中具有优势，但集成到多用户上行系统时面临定时失配（TM）这一关键挑战。与OFDM中残余定时偏移仅转化为相位旋转不同，基于时延的波形会出现资源的实际移位，其影响尚需深入研究。

Method: 作者证明上述波形可在时不变框架下通过统一的输入-输出关系加以统一，从而对上行资源分配中的TM进行统一分析。将TM的影响与OFDM在多普勒扩展下的参考损伤进行基准对比，并进一步单独评估多普勒扩展与TM对各波形的联合影响，同时考察BER性能及编码对干扰的缓解作用。

Result: 在存在TM时，干扰抑制与保护开销之间存在基本权衡。基于时延的分配中的TM与基于频率的分配中的多普勒扩展具有类比性。在考虑的场景中，基于时延的波形相比OFDM仍能保持净性能优势。编码可缓解由此产生的干扰。

Conclusion: 该研究建立了一个问题框架，为基于时延波形在实际多用户操作中的未来补偿与调度策略开发提供了动机和基础。

Abstract: The shift towards 6G wireless networks calls for advanced waveforms to support emerging applications such as ultra-reliable and massive machine-type communications. Among the proposed candidates, OTFS, ODDM, OTSM, and OCDM share the common feature of exploiting the delay domain for data transmission. While these waveforms offer significant advantages in high-mobility and sensing scenarios, their integration into multi-user uplink systems reveals a critical challenge: timing misalignment (TM). Unlike OFDM, where residual timing offsets translate into phase rotations, delay-based waveforms experience actual shifts of resources, whose impact we investigate in this work. We show that the aforementioned waveforms can be unified under a time-invariant setup through a common input--output relation, which enables a unified analysis of TM in uplink resource allocation. The results reveal a fundamental trade-off between interference mitigation and guard overhead in the presence of TM. We further benchmark the observed behavior against OFDM under Doppler spread as a reference impairment and highlight the analogy between TM in delay-based allocation and Doppler spread in frequency-based allocation. The results show that delay-based waveforms can maintain a net performance advantage over OFDM in the considered setup. Beyond this unified Doppler-free analysis, we evaluate the joint impact of Doppler spread and TM on each waveform individually. We also investigate BER performance and show how coding can mitigate the resulting interference. This study therefore establishes a problem framework that motivates the development of future compensation and scheduling strategies for practical multi-user operation of delay-based waveforms.

</details>


### [79] [Learning or Modeling? Virtual XL-RIS Observation Reconstruction for Near-Field Sensing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.17566)
*Tuo Wu,Jie Tang,Jiacheng Wang,Kangda Zhi,Maged Elkashlan,Matthew C. Valenti,Mérouane Debbah,Kenneth Tong*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 该论文研究被动XL-RIS近场感知中虚拟RIS观测(VOR)重建的两种先验选择：标定传播几何 vs 配对训练数据，提出OG-NF-SPR并与紧凑型、DenseNet型深度重建器对比。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 被动XL-RIS提供位置敏感孔径用于近场感知，但阵元级信号无法直接观测；从较少的基站测量中恢复VOR需要先验信息，而几何标定先验与数据驱动先验哪种更有效尚不明确。

Method: 针对统一感知算子与目标信号，提出离格近场稀疏参数化重建(OG-NF-SPR)：以反射路径投影进行位置初始化，再用贝叶斯估计所有路径增益并连续精化位置；并给出无需轨迹标签、利用时间相关性的因果扩展；同时构建紧凑型与DenseNet型深度重建器作对照。以近场UAV跟踪为示例进行受控仿真，分别考察噪声、位置分布偏移、标定误差与传播失配。

Result: 20 dB匹配传播下，OG-NF-SPR的重建NMSE较两个标称深度网络低约67%；低SNR时统计平均优于单一几何解释；在足够强的可重复失配下，同规模失配族训练网络优于标称物理估计器，而标称训练无此增益；时间正则将共享轨迹上的位置RMSE从0.653 m降至0.225 m。

Conclusion: 重建性能与测量质量、标定精度及训练覆盖度紧密相关，据此可判断何时几何先验可替代监督标签、何时补充数据更有益。

Abstract: A passive extremely large-scale reconfigurable intelligent surface (XL-RIS) offers a position-sensitive aperture for near-field sensing, but its element-wise signals are not directly observed. Recovering this virtual RIS observation (VOR) from fewer base-station measurements requires prior information. This paper investigates when calibrated propagation geometry or paired training data provides the more effective reconstruction prior. For a common sensing operator and target signal, we develop off-grid near-field sparse parametric reconstruction (OG-NF-SPR) and compare it with compact and DenseNet-based deep reconstructors. OG-NF-SPR uses reflected-path projection for position initialization, followed by Bayesian estimation of all path gains and continuous position refinement. A causal extension incorporates temporal correlation without trajectory labels. Controlled simulations use near-field UAV tracking as an illustration and separately examine noise, position-distribution shift, calibration error, and propagation mismatch. Under matched propagation at 20 dB, OG-NF-SPR achieves approximately 67% lower reconstruction NMSE than the two nominal deep networks. At low SNR, statistical averaging is more effective than selecting a single geometric explanation. Under sufficiently strong repeatable mismatch, a same-size network trained on the mismatch family outperforms the nominal physical estimator, whereas nominal training does not provide this benefit. Temporal regularization also reduces position RMSE from 0.653 m to 0.225 m on a shared trajectory. The results relate reconstruction performance to measurement quality, calibration accuracy, and training coverage, providing guidance on when geometry can replace supervised labels and when additional data are useful.

</details>


### [80] [Shuf-Scatter: Permutation-Based DLI Suppression for OFDM Ambient Backscatter](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.17828)
*Salim Chabani,Riadh Ouzane,Salah Eddine Zegrar,Huseyin Arslan*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 本文提出Shuf-Scatter方案，通过对反射的OFDM波形施加确定性置换，形成相关域特征，利用重排后向散射信号与直连分量的准正交性抑制直连链路干扰（DLI），实现无需专用频谱资源和瞬时BL CSI的可靠检测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在基于OFDM的环境反向散射通信（AmBC）系统中，直连链路干扰（DLI）仍是主要瓶颈，严重制约后向散射信号的可靠检测。

Method: 对反射OFDM波形施加确定性置换，生成区别于直连分量的相关域签名；接收机利用重排后向散射信号与直连分量的准正交性来抑制DLI。

Result: 分析与仿真结果表明，在强DLI条件下Shuf-Scatter具有更优的误码率（BER）性能，同时保持原有OFDM传输的完整性。

Conclusion: Shuf-Scatter可在不占用专用频谱资源、不需瞬时后向散射链路CSI的前提下有效缓解DLI并实现可靠检测，且不损害传统OFDM通信。

Abstract: Direct Link Interference (DLI) remains a major bottleneck in Orthogonal Frequency Division Multiplexing (OFDM)-based Ambient Backscatter Communication (AmBC) systems. This letter proposes Shuf-Scatter, a scheme that applies a deterministic permutation to the reflected OFDM waveform to create a distinct correlation-domain signature. By exploiting the quasi-orthogonality between the reshuffled backscatter signal and the direct-link component, the proposed receiver effectively mitigates DLI and enables reliable detection without requiring dedicated spectral resources, or instantaneous Backscatter-Link (BL) Channel State Information (CSI). Analytical and simulation results demonstrate that Shuf-Scatter achieves superior Bit Error Rate (BER) performance under strong DLI while preserving the integrity of the legacy OFDM transmission.

</details>


### [81] [Link Both Views: Cross-Image Routing Attention for Dual-Endpoint V2I Beam-Pair Power Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.18195)
*Shiliang Lu,Lu Bai,Ziwei Huang,Xiang Cheng*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 提出跨图像路由注意力(CIRA)与CiFormer，实现双端RGB图像的稀疏关联与特征互增强，用于V2I毫米波波束对功率矩阵预测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 单端感知只能获得传播信道的局部视图，需在链路两端进行链路中心感知以更完整刻画传播特性。

Method: 设计跨图像路由注意力(CIRA)对双端图像中传播相关特征进行稀疏关联与互增强；基于CIRA构建CiFormer，预测毫米波波束对功率矩阵。

Result: 相比最强基线，CiFormer降低平均绝对误差，并将top-1波束对选择准确率提升3.15个百分点；可视化与消融实验验证双端感知与选择性跨视角交互的有效性。

Conclusion: 双端感知与选择性跨视角交互能捕捉传播相关依赖，方法可推广至波束预测之外的更广泛无线任务。

Abstract: For sensing-aided communications, single-endpoint sensing offers only a partial view of propagation, motivating link-centric sensing across both endpoints. We propose cross-image routing attention (CIRA) for sparse association and mutual enhancement of propagation-relevant features from dual-endpoint RGB images. Building on CIRA, CiFormer predicts millimeter-wave (mmWave) beam-pair power matrices for vehicle-to-infrastructure (V2I) communications. Experiments show that CiFormer reduces mean absolute error and improves top-1 beam-pair selection accuracy by 3.15 percentage points over the strongest baseline. Visualizations and ablation studies support dual-endpoint sensing and selective cross-view interaction in capturing propagation-relevant dependencies, suggesting broader applications in wireless tasks beyond beam prediction.

</details>


### [82] [Overcoming Catastrophic Forgetting: Continual Learning for Radar Waveform Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.18404)
*Mohamed Rabie,Chinthana J. Panagamuwa,Konstantinos G. Kyriakopoulos*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 首次对雷达波形识别中的持续学习方法进行大规模基准评测，识别并组合有效机制以提升性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着新波形不断出现，已部署的雷达波形识别分类器需在不从头重训的情况下学习新波形，但会产生灾难性遗忘；而既有雷达领域研究仅在狭窄基准上评测少量方法，缺乏关于该方法选择与有效机制的实证依据。

Method: 构建持续学习大规模基准：在顺序十任务问题上评测18种持续学习方法，数据取自4个公开数据集、覆盖54种波形；设置任务增量（TIL）与类别增量（CIL）两种场景，以及单轮与多轮训练范式；对领先方法内部机制进行消融，并与完整方法配对比较，n=6训练种子，结论不确定时增至n=12，p值在同一方法族内做Holm-Bonferroni校正。

Result: 保留旧任务数据的方法在TIL与CIL中均领先；单纯回放在TIL下弥补了下限与实用上限间差距的77-88%，但在CIL下仅46-55%。消融显示若干机制带来较小但统计显著的增益；将这些机制组合后，TIL和CIL下领先F1分别提升3.4-5.5%和2.7-4.9%，且组合模型在所有信噪比下均超过所有基准方法。

Conclusion: 该工作为雷达波形识别系统的研究人员提供了机制层面的设计依据。

Abstract: Automated radar waveform recognition classifies the waveform and modulation type of a received signal. As new waveform variants appear over time, a deployed classifier must learn them without retraining from scratch. Catastrophic Forgetting occurs when learning new waveforms overwrites prior knowledge. Continual Learning (CL) methods tackle this challenge, but prior radar studies only evaluate a few methods on narrow benchmarks. Practitioners lack evidence on which method to deploy or which mechanisms are effective. This work presents the first large-scale benchmark of continual learning for radar waveform recognition. Eighteen CL methods are evaluated on a sequential ten-task problem, drawn from four public datasets spanning 54 waveforms, under task- and class-incremental (TIL, CIL) settings and single- and multi-epoch regimes. Methods that retain data from old tasks lead both TIL and CIL settings. Plain replay alone closes 77-88% of the gap between the lower and upper practical limits under TIL, but only 46-55% under CIL. The internal mechanisms of the leading methods are ablated and statistically tested to identify effective mechanisms. Each ablation is paired against its full method over n=6 training seeds, topped up to n=12 when inconclusive, and p-values are Holm-Bonferroni corrected within each method's family. Ablation results show that several mechanisms confer smaller but statistically significant gains. Combining these mechanisms improves the leading F1 score by 3.4-5.5% and 2.7-4.9% under TIL and CIL, respectively, and the combined models exceed every benchmarked method in both settings for all SNRs. These results give researchers of radar waveform classification systems a mechanism-level basis for design.

</details>


### [83] [A Three-Axis Rydberg Atomic Receiver Model for Polarization-Based Direction-of-Arrival Sensing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.18450)
*Bangyan Xia,Bo Tan*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 本文提出一种三轴里德堡原子接收机模型，用于射频信号源的到达方向（DoA）估计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将里德堡拍频矢量前端转化为高灵敏度的射频场DoA传感器，以突破传统矢量天线的探测能力限制。

Method: 采用三个名义正交的射频本振（LO），各赋不同频偏，将微弱入射射频场投影为光学读出中可分辨的复拍频系数；通过响应校准机制将系数映射到笛卡尔坐标系三轴，得到拍频矢量观测；对已知圆极化射频信号，该观测诱导出方向相关的里德堡导向矢量，进而实现最大似然DoA估计。

Result: 仿真结果表明，迹线级拍频提取能够支撑所提出的DoA感知流程，并为与传统矢量天线（VA）基准的比较提供基础。

Conclusion: 该方案将里德堡拍频矢量前端有效转化为高灵敏度射频场DoA传感器，验证了方法的可行性并建立了与经典矢量天线的对比框架。

Abstract: This paper develops a three-axis Rydberg atomic receiver model for direction-of-arrival (DoA) estimation of the radio frequency (RF) signal source. Three nominally orthogonal RF local oscillators (LOs), each assigned a distinct frequency offset, project the weak incident RF field into resolvable complex beat coefficients in the optical readout. A beat-vector observation is obtained by adopting a response calibration mechanism that maps the coefficients onto the three axes of the Cartesian coordinate system. For a known circularly polarised RF signal, this observation induces a direction-dependent Rydberg steering vector, enabling maximum-likelihood DoA estimation. The pro posed procedure translates the Rydberg beat-vector front end into a highly sensitive RF field DoA sensor. Simulation results show that trace-level beat extraction supports the proposed DoA sensing workflow and provides a basis for comparison with a traditional vector antenna (VA) benchmark.

</details>


### [84] [Task-evoked eye movements as candidate digital biomarkers of depression severity in adolescents and young adults](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.18494)
*Youxiang Sun,Yujie Nie,Zhuoru Wu,Mengyuan Yu,Wenkong Wang,Xiang Li,Yumei Wang,Xin Ma,Lizhou Fan*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 任务诱发眼动特征可作为青少年与青年抑郁严重程度的候选数字生物标志物，其机制偏向视觉探索与记忆引导注视控制异常，而非泛化的负性注意偏向。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统抑郁症状量表评估主观性强、时效性有限，需要可客观量化抑郁严重程度的数字指标，以辅助更及时的临床评估与决策支持。

Method: 单中心横断面研究，纳入155名14–20岁被试，完成情绪-空间自由观看与记忆引导回忆任务，按17项汉密尔顿抑郁量表分层；对19个预设眼动特征分属6个任务相关域，采用协变量调整的序数趋势模型分析；并补充机器学习分析，使用44个候选眼动特征与人口学协变量，比较单阶段、两阶段与三阶段级联分类器的四分类分层性能。

Result: 抑郁严重程度越高，注视分配熵越低（标准化β=-0.599）、区域间切换次数越少（β=-0.526）、首个目标进入潜伏期越长（β=0.572）、目标停留比例越低（β=-0.678），经FDR校正后q<0.001；情绪特异性注意偏向指标与严重程度无关联，注视离散度相关关联在调整焦虑症状后减弱。三阶段级联分类器四分类表现最佳：准确率0.665，宏F1 0.670，ROC曲线下面积0.869，优于单阶段与两阶段方法。

Conclusion: 任务诱发眼动可捕捉与抑郁严重程度相关的视觉探索及记忆引导注视控制改变，具备作为客观数字生物标志物的潜力，支持后续开展前瞻性研究，验证其在及时严重程度评估与临床决策支持中的补充价值。

Abstract: Objective digital measures of depression severity could complement conventional symptom assessments and facilitate more timely clinical evaluation. Here, we investigated whether task-evoked eye movements provide interpretable behavioral markers of depression severity in adolescents and young adults. In this single-center, cross-sectional study, 155 participants aged 14--20 years completed emotional-spatial free-viewing and memory-guided recall tasks and were stratified by 17-item Hamilton Depression Rating Scale scores. We examined 19 prespecified eye-movement features across six task-informed domains using covariate-adjusted ordinal-trend models. Greater depression severity was associated with lower gaze-allocation entropy (standardized $β=-0.599$), fewer inter-area switches ($β=-0.526$), longer first target-entry latency ($β=0.572$), and lower target dwell proportion ($β=-0.678$; all false-discovery-rate-adjusted $q<0.001$). Emotion-specific attentional-bias measures were not associated with severity, whereas associations involving fixation dispersion weakened after adjustment for anxiety symptoms. In a complementary machine-learning analysis incorporating 44 candidate eye-movement features and demographic covariates, a three-stage cascade classifier achieved the highest four-class stratification performance (accuracy, 0.665; macro-F1, 0.670; area under the receiver operating characteristic curve, 0.869), outperforming single-stage and two-stage approaches. Together, these findings identify altered visual exploration and memory-guided gaze control as candidate digital biomarkers of depression severity, rather than generalized negative attentional bias. These results support further prospective evaluation of task-evoked eye tracking as a complementary approach for timely severity assessment and clinical decision support.

</details>


### [85] [Contactless gait-derived features as candidate digital biomarkers of depression severity in adolescents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.18510)
*Shuai Liu,Xiang Li,Guangxin Dai,Mengyuan Yu,Wenkong Wang,Hanchi Liu,Jiahui Niu,Yujie Nie,Yumei Wang,Xin Ma,Lizhou Fan*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: Summary generation failed


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Motivation analysis unavailable

Method: Method extraction failed

Result: Result analysis unavailable

Conclusion: Conclusion extraction failed

Abstract: Timely assessment of depression severity in adolescents is essential for guiding clinical care, yet conventional evaluations rely on episodic clinical assessments and symptom rating scales. Contactless gait analysis offers a potentially scalable approach to capturing objective behavioral features associated with depression.
